# clawdpop

Suno + Claude Code music videos in the "I'm Upping My P(doom)" lineage (Suno "Claude-Pop"
song → forced-aligned lyrics → every frame a deterministic function of song time, painted
in a browser and exported through headless Chrome and ffmpeg).

## Songs

- `songs/2am-crying/` — "2AM (And I'm Crying)": untz-untz Eurodance from the point of view of
  a one-year-old. `LYRICS.md`, `TREATMENT.md`, `SUNO_RUNS.md`, `synth/` (procedural demo of
  the backing track), `video/` (renderer scenes).
- `songs/baby-facts/` — dense fact-rap about infant science (Netflix *Babies* and the
  literature behind it). `CONCEPT.md`, `FACTS.md`.

## Shared tooling

- `shared/renderer/` — song-agnostic engine: timeline, karaoke band, HUD, headless-Chrome
  frame export, ffmpeg encode.
- `shared/align/` — audio analysis: Demucs vocal stem → CTC forced alignment (torchaudio
  MMS_FA) cross-checked with mlx-whisper → `lyrics.json`; librosa beats/downbeats/sections →
  `audio.json`.

## Pipeline

1. Lyrics first. Dense, every line carrying a reference; a fixed hook that escalates.
2. Scope Suno runs: custom mode, lyrics with section markers, 2 to 3 style-tag variants.
   Driven with the `suno` CLI (`~/.local/bin/suno`, unofficial, costs ~70 credits per v5.5
   call). Auth is a manual one-time `suno auth --login`.
3. Reification with Opus 5.5 agents: procedural synth demo of the track (so the renderer can
   be built before Suno audio exists), renderer engine + plates, alignment pipeline.
4. Align the keeper Suno clip, swap the mock timing for real timing, render 1080p60.

Audio and video outputs are gitignored; code, specs, JSON timing and contact sheets are
committed.
----
# 2AM (And I'm Crying): the video

Treatment v3, "the baby's selfie video" (`../TREATMENT.md`), over the v3 lyric sheet
(`../suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`). Every frame is a pure function of song time, painted on a
canvas in headless Chrome and encoded with ffmpeg. The engine is song-agnostic and lives in
`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby (baby Clawd), props,
the caption and status-bar styling, the time-of-day grade and the edit rules.

Review surface: `sheets/plates.png` (one cell per clip, 9:16) and `sheets/layout-16x9.png`.

## Commands

All from the repo root (`/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop`). One-time setup:

```sh
npm install --prefix shared/renderer          # puppeteer-core only; drives the installed Chrome
```

Preview (scrub, play against the audio; space, arrows = beat, shift+arrows = bar, `[` `]` = clip):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs        # then open:
# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=23
# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?layout=16x9&t=40
```

Draft render over the Suno vocal (9:16, 540x960, 30 fps; about 7 min with the default 9 workers on this Mac):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video \
  --audio songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/it-s-2am-and-i-m-crying-bright-13e92be2.mp3 \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-suno-E2-clawd-540p.mp4 --check
# 16:9 draft: add --layout 16x9 (960x540) and a different --out
# mock timing over the synth demo: --data data-mock (and no --audio)
```

Final renders (1080p60; `A=songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/it-s-2am-and-i-m-crying-bright-13e92be2.mp3`):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --audio $A --w 1080 --h 1920 --fps 60 \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1080x1920-60.mp4
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --audio $A --layout 16x9 --w 1920 --h 1080 --fps 60 \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1920x1080-60.mp4
```

Contact sheets and stills:

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/plates.png
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --layout 16x9 \
  --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/layout-16x9.png --sheet-mode even --count 12 --cols 4 --cell-w 480
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --stills 4.5,40,77
node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --q t=23 --q "layout=16x9&t=40"   # preview page loads cleanly
```

Timing and placeholder audio:

```sh
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs --out songs/2am-crying/video/data-mock   # mock pair (data/ holds the Suno take)
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs --plan --out songs/2am-crying/video/data-mock   # ignore the synth, use the 128 BPM plan
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs      # audio/click.wav (fallback audio, gitignored)
```

`render.mjs` options: `--from/--to` seconds, `--workers N` (default ~60% of cores),
`--crf 16`, `--preset medium`, `--audio file`, `--data dir` (timing dir relative to the song dir,
default `data/`), `--force` (allow audio/timing length mismatch), `--check` (determinism: ordered vs
shuffled frames across workers; loop: outro frames equal intro frames; exit 2 on a failure). Video is libx264 CRF 16, yuv420p, tagged BT.709; audio AAC 192k.

## Data

Current timing: the Suno take **runE2 13e92be2** (`../audio/runE2/it-s-2am-and-i-m-crying-bright-13e92be2.mp3`,
195.56 s, 133.7 BPM), aligned by `shared/align` (`align.py` + `analyze.py`, output in
`../audio/runE2/data/`, copied here). The mock timing it replaced (synth demo / 128 BPM plan) is in
`data-mock/` (`?data=data-mock/` in the preview, `--data data-mock` in render.mjs).

- `data/lyrics.json`: `{ lines: [{ i, text, start, end, section, words: [{ w, start, end, conf, syl?, bv? }] }], extras, notes }`
  (mexicat/pdoom-video schema). **`section` is load-bearing**: the sheet header's id, `intro, chorus1, verse1,
  chorus2, verse2, bridge, final, outro` (the aligner writes it; naming in `shared/align/README.md`). Clip
  selection keys on it: verse clauses cut on the half bar, every `bridge` clause goes into the one long take,
  every `final` clause into the grid, and the `outro` "Hi" line is the loop.
- `data/audio.json`: `{ bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{ name, start, end }], kicks, duration }` plus
  optional fields: `beats_per_bar`, `hats` (offbeat onsets), `quiet` (silent spans), `risers`, `vocal_onsets`,
  `loudness`, `source` (`"synth (...)"`, `"plan"`, or a take with its file in parentheses; the preview
  plays that file, render.mjs finds the synth wav itself and otherwise needs `--audio`), `lyrics_sheet` (the sheet, relative to the song root `songs/2am-crying/`
  or to the repo root). analyze.py's `sections` are detected structure named `s1..s11`; nothing selects
  clips by them.

Section precedence in the engine (`shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js`): a line's own `section`, else the
section derived from the sheet named in `audio.json.lyrics_sheet` (same naming, matched by line text in
order; `[lyrics] ... derived from the lyric sheet` warning), else audio.json's sections (warning; with
`s1..s11` no section plate can match). render.mjs prints at start-up where the sections came from and
which section plates exist, and says `MISSING` for an absent one:

```
[render] line sections from lyrics.json: intro, chorus1, verse1, chorus2, verse2, bridge, final, outro
[render] section plate ok: bridge long take -> nap:long 2:06.13-2:24.09 (18.0s)
[render] section plate ok: final chorus grid -> grid 2:24.09-3:08.98 (44.9s)
[render] section plate ok: outro loop -> hello 3:08.98-3:15.56 (6.6s)
```

`--check` fails (exit 2) when the outro loop is missing or an outro frame differs from its intro frame.
The loop is anchored on the "Hi" line: outro frame k shows intro frame k - shift, the shift being the
whole-frame distance between the two "Hi"s (186.367 s on runE2; the outro window starts 0.18 s before its
"Hi", the intro 2.80 s before), so the caption stays on the vocal.

`tools/mock_timing.mjs` builds the mock pair (`--plan` ignores the synth); write it to `data-mock/`. Clips
are derived from lyric content, so a new take only needs its `lyrics.json`/`audio.json` in `data/`. Watch the
render log for `[clips] ... matched no rule` (a clause whose text drifted falls back to the closeup clip) and
`[timeline] ... moved off a shared downbeat` (two clauses snapped to the same downbeat; the later one moves to
the next beat).

Audio: for the Suno take pass it explicitly (`--audio songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/it-s-2am-and-i-m-crying-bright-13e92be2.mp3`;
the preview finds it from `source`). With the mock data the draft uses `../synth/out/2am_crying.wav`
when `audio.json.source` is the synth (the full-song file, chosen by duration); otherwise
`audio/click.wav`, a click + kick + hats + riser track generated from audio.json by
`tools/make_click.mjs`. render.mjs refuses audio whose length differs from audio.json by more than a bar.

## The character: baby Clawd

The lead is a baby Clawd, the Claude Code mascot (the protagonist of the "I'm Upping My P(doom)"
video), drawn fresh in Canvas 2D in the flat style. Adult Clawd is a 10 x 6 unit clay block on
four legs with two arm nubs and tall slit eyes; the baby is the same idea made younger:

- body: chubbier and nearly square (2.5r wide, 2.6r tall, r = the scene's `r`), rounder corners,
  the sides bowing out a little; terracotta clay `#d77757` with a light patch up and left, dark
  ink outline; a single curl on top; four short stubby legs in a darker clay.
- face (around `(o.x, o.y)`, where the old round head's centre was, so props still land): eyes
  are bigger, lower, rounded rectangles (0.31r x 0.46r at +-0.44r) with a big highlight dot;
  small mouth at +0.47r; blush always a little, more on demand (hatched above 0.6).
- the phone nub always stretches from the body's side to the bottom corner, widening toward the
  lens (the phone itself is never seen). The free nub: `down`, `wave`, `up`, `hold` (to a point
  the scene gives, where the scene draws the prop), `point`, `none`.
- outfits: `sack` (night: a soft bag over the lower body and the legs, zipped up the middle),
  `onesie` (day: a cloth band across the lower body with two snaps), `naked` (bare clay with
  Clawd's darker band, after the bath), `towel` (a cape behind).
- expressions on the same API as before: eyes `open` / `half` / continuous `open` (a heavy lid
  that droops toward the outer corner) / `closed` (a soft arc) / `squeeze` (> <) / `wide` / `focus`
  (a narrow slit); mouth `line` / `o` (the crying O, with a tongue when wide open) / `smile` /
  `grin` / `press` (a flat crimped line, implies a furrowed brow) / `none`; `tears` (thick streams
  and flicking drops, the value is the animation phase), `blush`, `flash`, `stain` (milk on the
  onesie and a dribble), `look` (eyes and highlights shift), `brows`, and the new `pacifier`
  (0..1: a shield and ring over the mouth; on for 5am quiet, 7:45 "sleeping", 8pm-12am sleeping,
  the 11am and 3pm naps).
- `BABY_GEOM` exports the geometry (the 5am / 1am glowing eyes in the dark use it).
- the parent is an adult Clawd (`props.adultClawd`): a backlit silhouette down the lit hall at
  3am, slumped by the crib at 4am (dim clay, tired eyes over the baby's shoulder), asleep in the
  armchair on the rear camera at 10pm (eyes shut, mouth open, z's); the 12pm spoon comes in on an
  adult nub; the 7:45 IR monitor cut-in shows baby Clawd in its sack.

Palette keys (`tod.js` `BASE`, re-lit by `grade()`, and the night `FLASH` / intro `SCREEN` sets):
`skin` (clay), `skinShade`, `skinLight`, `line`, `ink`, `mouth`, `blush`, `tear`, `paci`,
`paciRing`, `parentSkin`, `parentShade`.

## How it is put together

- `clips.js`: splits every lyric line into time clauses ("It's <time> ..."), maps each clause's
  text to a clip type + params (the rule table), and returns the timeline: one window per
  clause (cut on the downbeat; on the half-bar in the double-time verses, so two cuts per bar),
  one long-take window for the bridge, one grid window for the final chorus, and the outro.
- `story.js`: status-bar clock (the clause's time; minute by minute in the long take), battery
  (drains, charges with a bolt during naps, ends the day in the red), record timer, and the
  loop: outro frames are intro frames, quantized to the frame grid (`--check` verifies it).
- `song.js`: the compositor. Clip under the camera transform (handshake + a slight kick shake +
  per-clip tilt, owned here so a clip can be held still) -> lens post (grain on a fixed 24 Hz
  tick, vignette) -> lens overlays (splat, droplets, finger) -> caption -> status bar; then the
  layout: full frame (9:16) or the phone centred on a soft nursery backdrop with a sun/moon
  arc (16:9).
- `tod.js`: time-of-day light (deep blue night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm,
  blue 8pm), the night flash palette, the phone-screen glow.
- `baby.js` (baby Clawd), `props.js` (rooms, props, the adult Clawd parent), `monitor.js` (the v1
  IR monitor view, cut into 7:45 "sleeping").
- `scenes/`: one module per clip type: `hello`, `crib`, `nap`, `pooping`, `closeup`, `highchair`,
  `standing`, `fan`, `bath`, `rear`, `grid`.

## Deliberate departures

- The baby is baby Clawd, not the treatment's round-headed baby ("the baby is... not that cute?
  have we considered baby clawd?"). Its arms are Clawd's nubs, so the phone arm is a stretched
  nub rather than an upper arm and elbow; the parent is an adult Clawd.

- Verse 2 is 9 bars (the brief's 8 plus the bar of silence after "1am and I'm..."), 57 bars total.
- No wide-angle edge stretch: it needs a per-frame offscreen remap for an effect that is barely
  visible at phone size. Day "over-saturation" is carried by the time-of-day grade instead.
- The 12th grid tile (11 final-chorus lines in a 3x4 grid) is the record button rather than a clip.
- The drinking clips hold the bottle with one hand: the other hand is always on the phone.

# Treatment v3: the baby's selfie video

Supersedes `TREATMENT-v1-monitor.md` (the nursery-camera concept; still a good source for
the night clips and the baby character). Lyrics: `LYRICS.md` → "v3: the day", and
`suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`.

## Concept

The baby is filming a day-in-my-life video on a phone, front camera, in the style of the
"Imma Be" selfie trend: one clip per timestamp, a caption in the corner that reads exactly
what the lyric says, hard cuts on the beat, and the day looping back to 2am at the end.
Every line of the song is "It's [time] and I'm [doing]", so every line is one clip and the
caption is the lyric. The clock is still the escalating number, but now it lives in the
phone's status bar and in the caption.

Tone: the baby is sincere. It is not performing misery; it is documenting. The phone is
held badly (too close, tilted, thumb on the lens once), which is where the charm comes from.

## Formats

- Primary: vertical 1080x1920 (9:16), the trend's native shape. 60 fps final, 30 fps drafts.
- Secondary: 1920x1080 (16:9) with the vertical phone frame centered on a soft nursery
  backdrop (the room out of focus, time-of-day lighting), for YouTube. Same scenes, one
  layout switch.

## Style bible

- **Look.** A phone front camera: slight wide-angle stretch at the edges, mild over-
  saturation by day, grain and a hard flash at night (the flash lights the baby's face and
  nothing else). Not the IR greyscale of v1, except as a joke for the 7:45pm "sleeping" clip,
  where the parents' monitor view is cut in.
- **Caption.** The trend style: lowercase, heavy grotesque (Archivo Black / Inter 900),
  white with a black outline or on a rounded black pill, upper third of the frame. The
  caption is the lyric line, revealed word by word in sync, and stays until the cut. Times
  are digits ("it's 2am and i'm crying").
- **Status bar.** A thin phone status bar at the top: the clock (which is the escalation
  number), battery draining across the day and charging during naps, a red record dot
  with a running timer.
- **Baby.** The same procedural character as v1: round head, two dot eyes, a few hairs,
  stubby arms; sleep sack at night, onesie by day. One arm always extends toward the bottom
  corner of the frame, holding the phone (we see the upper arm and the elbow, not the phone).
  Expressions are tiny: eyes open/closed/half, mouth a line, an O, or a small smile.
- **Time of day.** Background colour temperature and brightness follow the clock: deep blue
  night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm, blue again at 8pm. A tiny sun/moon
  arc in the backdrop of the 16:9 layout.
- **Motion.** Cuts on downbeats. In the double-time verses, two cuts per bar. The phone
  shakes with the kick, slightly. Slams on "pooping again". No cute wobble beyond the
  selfie handshake.
- **Bans.** No photos, no real faces, no emoji-style faces, no diaper-contents humour.
  "Pooping" is a face and a caption, nothing else.

## Clips

Each clip is anchored to its lyric line by content. Duration is whatever the line's words
cover, snapped to the beat grid.

| line | clip |
|---|---|
| intro "Hi. It's me." | Phone wakes; the baby's face too close, then pulls back; a wave; record dot starts. |
| 2am crying | Dark crib, flash on, mouth an O, eyes squeezed; the caption's "crying" slams. |
| 3am crying | Same framing, phone tilted 15°, a parent silhouette appearing in the doorway behind. |
| 4am crying | Same framing, the parent silhouette now sitting on the floor next to the crib. |
| 11am napping | Hard cut to bright daylight, eyes closed, the phone slowly tipping over to the ceiling fan. |
| 12pm crying | High chair, daylight, a spoon mid-air. |
| 1pm pooping | Concentration face. Caption only. Hold absolutely still. |
| 2pm pooping again | Identical frame to 1pm; the caption gets "again" slammed on in red. |
| 5am quiet | Nearly black; just the record dot and two open eyes. |
| 5:45 up | Flash; the baby standing, grinning; the parents' alarm clock visible behind: 5:45. |
| 6am standing / 6:15 still standing | Same frame twice, the second with a timer overlay. |
| 7am drinking / 7:30 wearing it | Bottle with both hands, eyes half closed; then a milk-stained onesie, proud. |
| 8am eating / 8:05 throwing it | High chair; then food arcing past the lens, a splat on the glass. |
| 9am reading / 9:01 eating it | Board book held up; then the book's corner in the mouth. |
| 10am pointing / 10:30 pointing at the fan | Finger into the lens; then camera whips up to the ceiling fan. |
| 10:45 fighting it | Eyes closing and snapping open, twice, on the beat. |
| 3pm napping | Daylight nap in a car seat or stroller, head at an angle. |
| 4pm drinking | Bottle, golden light. |
| 5pm crying / 5:30 crying at the fan | Witching hour; then the fan again, accusingly. |
| 6pm eating / 6:05 the floor is eating | High chair; then camera tilts down: food on the floor, caption deadpan. |
| 7pm splashing | Bath; water droplets on the lens; the caption drips. |
| 7:15 naked and running | Motion blur, towel cape, the frame shaking, laughter. |
| 7:30 reading / 7:45 "sleeping" | Book in the crib; then dark room, eyes wide open, caption with quotation marks; a one-second cut-in of the v1 IR monitor view. |
| 8pm, 9pm sleeping | Dark, eyes closed, the phone drifting. |
| 10pm you're sleeping | Camera flips to the rear: a parent asleep in the chair, mouth open. The only rear-camera shot. |
| 11pm we're sleeping / 12am sleeping | Darker, slower, the record timer the only motion. |
| 1am and I'm... | Black. Record dot. One bar of silence. Eyes open. |
| bridge 10:50 → 11am | The one long take: bottle, eyes heavy, the phone tilting degree by degree until it lies flat looking at the ceiling fan; the fan's blades slow with the half-time. |
| final chorus | The day as a filling grid of all the clips, 3 wide, each tile lighting on its line, until the 1am tile goes black. |
| outro "Hi. It's me." | The intro clip again, frame-exact, so the video loops. |

## Technical notes

Same engine contract as before: deterministic frames, `data/lyrics.json` word timings,
`data/audio.json` beat grid and sections. One scene module per clip type (crib-night,
high-chair, bath, standing, nap, pooping-face, fan, rear-camera, grid), parameterised by
time-of-day and caption, instead of one module per lyric line. The caption component and
the status bar are shared. A `layout` switch selects 9:16 or 16:9.

     1	// Naps. mode 'tip' (11am): hard cut to daylight, eyes closed, the phone slowly tipping
     2	// over until it looks up at the ceiling fan. mode 'carseat' (3pm): head at an angle in the
     3	// car seat. mode 'long' (the bridge, one long take): bottle, eyes getting heavy, the phone
     4	// slipping a degree each beat, then tipping flat onto the ceiling fan, whose blades slow
     5	// with the half-time.
     6	import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
     7	import { ceiling, cribBack, fillAll, bottle, carSeat, carSeatStraps, windowPane } from '../props.js';
     8	import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, wordT, CW, CH } from './common.js';
     9	import { lerp, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
    10	import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
    11	
    12	const BAND = 320;          // wall + crib rail between the face and the ceiling
    13	const UP = CH + BAND;      // the ceiling frame sits one frame (plus the band) above the baby
    14	
    15	/** The tip-over strip: lying baby (y >= 0), a wall band, then the ceiling frame above. */
    16	function tipStack(ctx, C, drawLying, fan) {
    17	  const { G } = C;
    18	  ctx.save();
    19	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, 0, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
    20	  drawLying();
    21	  ctx.restore();
    22	  // band: the crib's far rail against the wall
    23	  ctx.fillStyle = G.wall;
    24	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -BAND, CW + 1800, BAND);
    25	  ctx.fillStyle = G.crib;
    26	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -110, CW + 1800, 80);
    27	  for (let x = -600; x < CW + 600; x += 110) ctx.fillRect(x, -60, 40, 60);
    28	  ctx.save();
    29	  ctx.translate(0, -UP);
    30	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
    31	  ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 900, R: 520, ...fan });
    32	  ctx.restore();
    33	}
    34	
    35	/** Fan angle with speed ramping v0 -> v1 over [a, b] (analytic, so frames stay independent). */
    36	function fanAngle(t, a, b, v0, v1) {
    37	  if (t <= a) return v0 * t;
    38	  const base = v0 * a;
    39	  if (t <= b) { const d = t - a; return base + v0 * d + ((v1 - v0) * d * d) / (2 * (b - a)); }
    40	  return base + v0 * (b - a) + ((v1 - v0) * (b - a)) / 2 + v1 * (t - b);
    41	}
    42	
    43	function lyingBaby(ctx, C, o = {}) {
    44	  // the baby on its back on the crib sheet, seen from the phone held above its face
    45	  const { G } = C;
    46	  fillAll(ctx, G.sheet);
    47	  // sheet folds
    48	  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(G.sheetShade, 0.55);
    49	  ctx.lineWidth = 10;
    50	  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-200 + i * 260, 300); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-60 + i * 260, 900, -220 + i * 260, 1900); ctx.stroke(); }
    51	  drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line', ...o });
    52	}

    54	export default {
    55	  camera(C) {
    56	    const p = C.params;
    57	    if (p.mode === 'carseat') return { shake: 0.6, kick: 0.4, tilt: 0.04 * Math.sin(C.t * 7) * 0.2 };
    58	    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
    59	      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.15, C.end - C.dur * 0.05));
    60	      return { dy: UP * 0.97 * k, tilt: 0.3 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k) + 0.12 * k, zoom: 1 - 0.1 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: 0.4, kick: 0.25 };
    61	    }
    62	    // long take: a degree per beat, then the tip-over at "11am"
    63	    const elev = C.story.clauses.find((c) => c.section === 'bridge' && /11am/.test(c.n));
    64	    const tipA = elev ? C.B.snap(elev.start, 'downbeat') : C.start + C.dur * 0.7;
    65	    const tipB = Math.min(C.end - C.B.barDur * 0.5, tipA + C.B.barDur * 2);
    66	    const beats = Math.max(0, C.B.beatIndex(Math.min(C.t, tipA)) - C.B.beatIndex(C.start));
    67	    const slip = Math.floor(beats) + ease.outCubic(beats % 1);
    68	    const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, tipA, tipB));
    69	    return { dy: UP * 0.97 * k, tilt: 0.022 * slip * (1 - k) + 0.1 * k + 0.25 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), zoom: 1 - 0.08 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: 0.35, kick: 0 };
    70	  },
    71	  caption(C) {
    72	    return C.params.mode === 'long' ? { family: '"Inter", sans-serif' } : {};
    73	  },
    74	  draw(ctx, C) {
    75	    const p = C.params;
    76	    const { G } = C;
    77	    if (p.mode === 'carseat') {
    78	      // car window behind, daylight, the seat shell around the head
    79	      fillAll(ctx, mix(G.seatShade, '#000000', 0.2));
    80	      windowPane(ctx, G, { x: -80, y: 120, w: 1240, h: 640, curtains: false });
    81	      const tilt = 0.38;
    82	      carSeat(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x, y: SELFIE.y, r: SELFIE.r, tilt });
    83	      const x = SELFIE.x + 60, y = SELFIE.y + 30;
    84	      drawBaby(ctx, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.25, pacifier: 1, tilt });
    85	      carSeatStraps(ctx, G, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r, tilt });
    86	      return;
    87	    }
    88	    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
    89	      tipStack(ctx, C, () => lyingBaby(ctx, C, { pacifier: 1 }), { angle: C.t * 3.2, blur: 0.7 });
    90	      return;
    91	    }
    92	    // ---- the long take -----------------------------------------------------------
    93	    const cl = C.story.clauses.filter((c) => c.section === 'bridge');
    94	    const at = (re, f) => (cl.find((c) => re.test(c.n)) || {}).start ?? f;
    95	    const tWarm = at(/10:55/, C.start + C.dur * 0.25);
    96	    const tHeavy = at(/10:58/, C.start + C.dur * 0.5);
    97	    const tNap = at(/11am/, C.start + C.dur * 0.7);
    98	    const heavy = wordT(C, 'heavy', tWarm + 1.2);
    99	    const open = 1 - 0.45 * ease.inOutQuad(seg(C.t, tWarm, heavy + 0.4)) - 0.55 * ease.inOutQuad(seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap));
   100	    const blink = C.t < tHeavy ? 0 : 0.35 * Math.max(0, Math.sin((C.t - tHeavy) * 5)) * (1 - seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap));
   101	    const droop = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap + 0.6));
   102	    const v0 = 9.0, v1 = 1.4;
   103	    const ang = fanAngle(C.t - C.start, tHeavy - C.start, C.end - C.start, v0, v1);
   104	    const speed = lerp(v0, v1, seg(C.t, tHeavy, C.end));
   105	    tipStack(ctx, C, () => {
   106	      lyingBaby(ctx, C, {
   107	        eyes: 'open', open: clamp(open - blink), look: [0, -0.3 * (1 - droop)], mouth: droop > 0.6 ? 'o' : 'none', mouthOpen: 0.25,
   108	        free: droop < 0.85 ? { mode: 'hold', at: [lerp(0.55, 1.2, droop), lerp(1.25, 2.0, droop)] } : { mode: 'down' },
   109	      });
   110	      // the bottle: at the mouth, then sliding off to the side as the hands go heavy
   111	      if (droop < 0.95) {
   112	        bottle(ctx, G, {
   113	          x: SELFIE.x + lerp(75, 330, droop), y: SELFIE.y + lerp(268, 560, droop), a: lerp(-0.52, -0.1, droop), s: 1.05,
   114	          level: lerp(0.6, 0.15, seg(C.t, C.start, tHeavy)),
   115	        });
   116	      }
   117	      // warmth: a soft sun patch on the sheet
   118	      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(300, 700, 50, 300, 700, 900);
   119	      g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#fff3c4', 0.28));
   120	      g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#fff3c4', 0));
   121	      ctx.fillStyle = g;
   122	      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
   123	    }, { angle: ang, blur: clamp(speed / 8) });
   124	  },
   125	};
     1	// Clips from lyric content. Every "It's <time> and I'm <doing>" clause is one clip; the
     2	// rule table maps the clause's words to a clip TYPE (scene module) and its params. The
     3	// bridge is one long take, the final chorus one grid, the outro is the intro again.
     4	
     5	import { norm } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';
     6	
     7	const HM = (h, m = 0) => h * 60 + m;
     8	
     9	/** "2am" / "12pm" / "5:45" / "10:30" -> minutes since midnight. am/pm inferred for h:mm. */
    10	export function minutesOf(text, prevMin = null) {
    11	  const s = String(text).toLowerCase();
    12	  let m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*([ap])\.?\s*m\b/);
    13	  if (m) { let h = +m[1] % 12; if (m[2] === 'p') h += 12; return HM(h); }
    14	  m = s.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
    15	  if (m) {
    16	    // h:mm without am/pm: take the reading closest after the previous clause's time
    17	    const h = +m[1] % 12, mm = +m[2];
    18	    const am = HM(h, mm), pm = HM(h + 12, mm);
    19	    if (prevMin == null) return am;
    20	    const fwd = (x) => (x - prevMin + 1440) % 1440;
    21	    return fwd(am) <= fwd(pm) ? am : pm;
    22	  }
    23	  return null;
    24	}
    25	
    26	/** Split lyric lines into time clauses: a clause starts at "It's" followed by a word with a digit. */
    27	export function clausesOf(L) {
    28	  const out = [];
    29	  let prevMin = null;
    30	  for (const line of L.lines) {
    31	    let cur = null;
    32	    line.words.forEach((w, k) => {
    33	      const next = line.words[k + 1];
    34	      const starts = norm(w.w) === 'its' && next && /\d/.test(next.w) && !w.backing;
    35	      if (starts) {
    36	        cur = { words: [], line, section: line.section, start: w.start };
    37	        out.push(cur);
    38	      }
    39	      if (cur) cur.words.push(w);
    40	      else if (out.length && out.at(-1).section === line.section && !/^(intro|outro)$/.test(line.section)) out.at(-1).words.push(w);
    41	    });
    42	  }
    43	  for (const c of out) {
    44	    c.text = c.words.filter((w) => !w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' ');
    45	    c.n = norm(c.text);
    46	    c.end = c.words.at(-1).end;
    47	    c.min = minutesOf(c.text, prevMin);
    48	    prevMin = c.min;
    49	    c.verb = c.n.replace(/^its \S+ (and )?/, '');
    50	  }
    51	  out.forEach((c, i) => { c.index = i; });
    52	  return out;
    53	}
    54	
    55	// [pattern on the normalized clause, type, params]. First match wins.

    87	  [/^its 10am and im pointing/, 'closeup', { prop: 'point' }],
    88	  [/^its 10:45 and im fighting it/, 'closeup', { mode: 'fight' }],
    89	  [/^its 5pm and im crying/, 'closeup', { mode: 'cry' }],
    90	];
    91	
    92	export function ruleFor(clause) {
    93	  for (const [re, type, params] of RULES) if (re.test(clause.n)) return { type, params: { ...params }, matched: String(re) };
    94	  return { type: 'closeup', params: {}, matched: null };
    95	}
    96	
    97	/**
    98	 * Plates that exist only because of a line's section id. If the sections are wrong (e.g.
    99	 * audio.json's detected s1..s11 instead of the sheet's ids) they silently vanish, so
   100	 * render.mjs lists them at start-up and flags any that are MISSING.
   101	 */
   102	export const SECTION_PLATES = [
   103	  { label: 'bridge long take', section: 'bridge', plate: 'nap' },
   104	  { label: 'final chorus grid', section: 'final', plate: 'grid' },
   105	  { label: 'outro loop', section: 'outro', plate: 'hello' },
   106	];
   107	
   108	/** Timeline spec function: the engine calls it with the parsed lyrics and beat grid. */
   109	export function timeline({ lyrics, beats }) {
   110	  const clauses = clausesOf(lyrics);
   111	  const entries = [{ plate: 'hello', at: { time: 0 }, params: { loop: false }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 }];
   112	  const hero80 = (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.8;
   113	  const fallbacks = [];
   114	  for (const c of clauses) {
   115	    if (c.section === 'bridge') {
   116	      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'nap' && e.params.mode === 'long')) {
   117	        entries.push({ plate: 'nap', params: { mode: 'long', clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'bridge').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'bridge long take' }, fromSection: 'bridge long take', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.5 });
   118	      }
   119	      continue;
   120	    }
   121	    if (c.section === 'final') {
   122	      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'grid')) {
   123	        entries.push({ plate: 'grid', params: { clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'final').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'final chorus grid' }, fromSection: 'final chorus grid', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.55 });
   124	      }
   125	      continue;
   126	    }
   127	    const rule = ruleFor(c);
   128	    if (!rule.matched) fallbacks.push(c.text);
   129	    entries.push({
   130	      plate: rule.type,
   131	      params: { ...rule.params, clause: c.index },
   132	      at: { t: c.start, why: c.text },
   133	      snap: /^verse/.test(c.section) ? 'halfbar' : 'downbeat',
   134	      hero: hero80,
   135	    });
   136	  }
   137	  const outro = lyrics.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
   138	  if (outro) entries.push({ plate: 'hello', params: { loop: true }, at: { line: 'Hi', section: 'outro', why: 'outro loop' }, fromSection: 'outro loop', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 });
   139	  if (fallbacks.length) console.warn(`[clips] ${fallbacks.length} clause(s) matched no rule and use the closeup fallback:\n  ${fallbacks.join('\n  ')}`);
   140	  const got = SECTION_PLATES.map((sp) => ({ ...sp, e: entries.find((e) => e.fromSection === sp.label) }));
   141	  const lost = got.filter((g) => !g.e);
   142	  if (lost.length) {
   143	    const have = [...new Set(lyrics.lines.map((l) => l.section))].join(', ');
   144	    console.warn(`[clips] MISSING section plate(s): ${lost.map((g) => `${g.label} (no line in section "${g.section}")`).join('; ')}. Line sections: ${have}`);
   145	  }
   146	  return entries;
   147	}

     1	// Baby Clawd: the Claude Code mascot as a baby. Clawd is a blocky clay body (10 units wide,
     2	// 6 tall) on four stubby legs with two arm nubs and tall rounded-rectangle eyes; the baby is
     3	// chubbier and nearly square, with rounder corners, shorter legs, bigger and lower eyes with
     4	// a big highlight, a single curl on top and blush. Night: a sleep sack that wraps the legs
     5	// (a soft bag with a zipper). Day: a onesie band across the lower body with two snaps.
     6	// 'naked' is the bare clay body (after the bath), 'towel' adds a cape. A pacifier for the
     7	// quiet and sleeping moments. One arm nub always stretches toward a bottom corner of the
     8	// frame, holding the phone (we see the nub, never the phone).
     9	//
    10	// drawBaby(ctx, o): o.x, o.y face centre, o.r scale (design px; the body is 2.5r wide), o.C
    11	// colours (skin = clay, skinShade, skinLight, ink, line, mouth, white, cloth, clothShade,
    12	// towel, stain, blush, tear, paci), plus the pose fields in BABY_DEFAULTS. No state, no
    13	// randomness: the same o always draws the same pixels.

    16	import { mix, rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
    17	
    18	export const BABY_DEFAULTS = {
    19	  r: 300, tilt: 0,
    20	  outfit: 'onesie',          // 'onesie' | 'sack' | 'naked' | 'towel'
    21	  eyes: 'open',              // 'open' | 'half' | 'closed' | 'squeeze' | 'wide' | 'focus'
    22	  open: 1,                   // continuous eyelid 0..1 (overrides when < 1 for 'open'/'wide')
    23	  look: [0, 0],              // eye / highlight offset (-1..1)
    24	  mouth: 'line',             // 'line' | 'o' | 'smile' | 'grin' | 'none' | 'press'
    25	  mouthOpen: 1,
    26	  brows: null,               // null | 'furrow' | 'up'  ('press' implies 'furrow')
    27	  phoneArm: 'left',          // which bottom corner the phone nub reaches for
    28	  free: { mode: 'down' },    // free nub: 'down' | 'wave' | 'up' | 'point' | 'hold' | 'none'
    29	  stain: false, tears: 0, blush: 0,
    30	  pacifier: 0,               // 0..1: the pacifier over the mouth (quiet and sleeping moments)
    31	  flash: 0,                  // 0..1 hot-spot of the phone flash on the face
    32	};
    33	
    34	/** Geometry in units of r around the face centre (scenes place props and glows with it). */
    35	export const BABY_GEOM = {
    36	  body: { l: -1.25, r: 1.25, t: -1.0, b: 1.6, rad: 0.56, bulge: 0.05 },
    37	  eye: { dx: 0.44, dy: 0.0, w: 0.31, h: 0.46, rad: 0.125 },
    38	  mouthY: 0.47,
    39	  cheek: { dx: 0.8, dy: 0.36 },
    40	  arm: { x: 1.16, y: 0.46, t: 0.32 },
    41	  legs: [-0.86, -0.38, 0.38, 0.86], legW: 0.3, legH: 0.3,
    42	  sack: { t: 0.74, b: 2.08, w: 1.33 },
    43	  band: 0.95,
    44	};
    45	const G0 = BABY_GEOM;

    47	const ROSE = '#ee8a8a', SKY = '#8fd0f6';
    48	
    49	/** The body outline: a rounded square whose sides bow out a little (chubby). */
    50	function bodyPath(ctx, r) {
    51	  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = G0.body;
    52	  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
    53	  ctx.beginPath();
    54	  ctx.moveTo(L + k, T);
    55	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, T - q, R - k, T);
    56	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
    57	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, (T + B) / 2, R, B - k);
    58	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, B, R - k, B);
    59	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, B + q, L + k, B);
    60	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, B, L, B - k);
    61	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, (T + B) / 2, L, T + k);
    62	  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
    63	  ctx.closePath();
    64	}
    65	
    66	function rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, k) {
    67	  k = Math.max(0, Math.min(k, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2));
    68	  ctx.beginPath();
    69	  ctx.moveTo(x + k, y);
    70	  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, k);
    71	  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, k);
    72	  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, k);
    73	  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, k);
    74	  ctx.closePath();
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * A nub from `a` to `b` (thickness t0 at the root, t1 at the tip). The root end sits inside
    79	 * the body and is not outlined, so the nub grows out of the body silhouette.
    80	 */
    81	function nub(ctx, a, b, t0, t1, fill, line, lw, { tipRound = 0.35, rootOpen = true } = {}) {
    82	  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    83	  const nx = -dy / d, ny = dx / d;
    84	  const h0 = t0 / 2, h1 = t1 / 2, c = Math.min(h1 * 2 * tipRound, d * 0.4);
    85	  const ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d;
    86	  const p = (s, h, along = 0) => [a[0] + ux * s * d + nx * h + ux * along, a[1] + uy * s * d + ny * h + uy * along];
    87	  const A = p(0, h0), Bq = p(1, h1, -c), Cq = p(1, -h1, -c), D = p(0, -h0);
    88	  const T1 = p(1, h1), T2 = p(1, -h1);
    89	  const outline = () => {
    90	    ctx.moveTo(A[0], A[1]);
    91	    ctx.lineTo(Bq[0], Bq[1]);
    92	    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T1[0], T1[1], (T1[0] + T2[0]) / 2, (T1[1] + T2[1]) / 2);
    93	    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T2[0], T2[1], Cq[0], Cq[1]);
    94	    ctx.lineTo(D[0], D[1]);
    95	  };
    96	  ctx.beginPath(); outline(); ctx.closePath();
    97	  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
    98	  if (line) {
    99	    ctx.beginPath(); outline();
   100	    if (!rootOpen) ctx.closePath();
   101	    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
   102	  }
   103	}
   104	
   105	function eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r) {
   106	  const E = G0.eye;
   107	  let mode = o.eyes;
   108	  const open = clamp(o.open);
   109	  if ((mode === 'open' || mode === 'wide' || mode === 'half') && open < 0.18) mode = 'closed';
   110	  const lx = o.look[0] * 0.07 * r, ly = o.look[1] * 0.06 * r;
   111	  const ex = k * E.dx * r, ey = E.dy * r;
   112	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.fillStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   113	  if (mode === 'closed') {
   114	    // sleeping: a soft downward arc
   115	    ctx.lineWidth = 0.055 * r;
   116	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.15 * r, ex + 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
   117	    return;
   118	  }
   119	  if (mode === 'squeeze') {
   120	    // squeezed shut: > <
   121	    ctx.lineWidth = 0.062 * r;
   122	    const w = 0.13 * r, h = 0.135 * r;
   123	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex + k * w, ey - h); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * w * 0.9, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + k * w, ey + h); ctx.stroke();
   124	    return;
   125	  }
   126	  if (mode === 'focus') {
   127	    // concentration: a narrow flat slit
   128	    rr(ctx, ex - 0.16 * r + lx * 0.5, ey + 0.02 * r, 0.32 * r, 0.12 * r, 0.05 * r); ctx.fill();
   129	    return;
   130	  }
   131	  const s = mode === 'wide' ? 1.14 : 1;
   132	  const w = E.w * s * r, h = E.h * s * r;
   133	  const x0 = ex - w / 2 + lx, y0 = ey - h / 2 + ly;
   134	  const lid = mode === 'half' ? Math.min(open, 0.5) : open;
   135	  const cut = y0 + h * (1 - lid);
   136	  // a heavy lid: its edge bows up a little and droops toward the outer corner (drowsy, not cross)
   137	  const droop = 0.09 * r * (1 - lid);
   138	  const lidY = (x) => cut - 0.03 * r * (1 - lid) + droop * ((x - ex) * k) / w - 0.04 * r * (1 - lid) * (1 - ((x - ex) / (w * 0.6)) ** 2);
   139	  ctx.save();
   140	  if (lid < 1) {
   141	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0 - 0.1 * r, lidY(x0 - 0.1 * r));
   142	    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) { const x = x0 - 0.1 * r + (w + 0.2 * r) * i / 8; ctx.lineTo(x, lidY(x)); }
   143	    ctx.lineTo(x0 + w + 0.1 * r, y0 + 2 * h); ctx.lineTo(x0 - 0.1 * r, y0 + 2 * h); ctx.closePath(); ctx.clip();
   144	  }
   145	  rr(ctx, x0, y0, w, h, E.rad * s * r); ctx.fill();
   146	  // the highlight: a big dot up and to the left, a small one below it
   147	  const hx = x0 + w * 0.33 + o.look[0] * 0.02 * r, hy = Math.max(y0 + h * 0.27, lid < 1 ? lidY(x0 + w * 0.33) + w * 0.3 : -1e9) + o.look[1] * 0.02 * r;
   148	  ctx.fillStyle = C.white || '#fff';
   149	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx, hy, w * 0.26, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   150	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.7, y0 + h * 0.68, w * 0.11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   151	  if (mode === 'wide') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.68, y0 + h * 0.3, w * 0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   152	  ctx.restore();
   153	  if (lid < 1) {
   154	    // the eyelid's edge, sagging a little
   155	    ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r;
   156	    ctx.beginPath();
   157	    for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) { const x = x0 - 0.05 * r + (w + 0.1 * r) * i / 8; if (i) ctx.lineTo(x, lidY(x)); else ctx.moveTo(x, lidY(x)); }
   158	    ctx.stroke();
   159	  }
   160	}

   162	function brow(ctx, k, kind, C, r) {
   163	  const E = G0.eye;
   164	  const ex = k * E.dx * r, by = (E.dy - E.h / 2 - 0.11) * r;
   165	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   166	  ctx.beginPath();
   167	  if (kind === 'furrow') { ctx.moveTo(ex + k * 0.16 * r, by - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * 0.1 * r, by + 0.04 * r); }
   168	  else { ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, by - 0.07 * r, ex + 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); }
   169	  ctx.stroke();
   170	}

   172	function tears(ctx, phase, C, r) {
   173	  const E = G0.eye;
   174	  const col = C.tear || SKY;
   175	  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
   176	    const x0 = k * (E.dx + 0.02) * r, y0 = (E.dy + E.h / 2 - 0.04) * r, y1 = 0.9 * r;
   177	    const pts = [];
   178	    for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) {
   179	      const f = i / 6;
   180	      pts.push([x0 + k * 0.05 * r * f + Math.sin(phase * TAU + i * 1.3 + k) * 0.022 * r * f, lerp(y0, y1, f)]);
   181	    }
   182	    const stroke = (w, c) => {
   183	      ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.lineWidth = w; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   184	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
   185	      for (const p of pts.slice(1)) ctx.lineTo(p[0], p[1]);
   186	      ctx.stroke();
   187	    };
   188	    stroke(0.13 * r, rgba(C.line, 0.6));
   189	    stroke(0.1 * r, col);
   190	    stroke(0.032 * r, rgba('#ffffff', 0.7));
   191	    // a drop flicking off to the side
   192	    for (const off of [0, 0.5]) {
   193	      const ph = (phase * 1.6 + off + (k > 0 ? 0.25 : 0)) % 1;
   194	      const dx = k * (0.22 + 0.5 * ph) * r, dy = (0.08 - 0.28 * Math.sin(Math.PI * ph) + 0.55 * ph * ph) * r;
   195	      const s = (1 - 0.45 * ph) * r;
   196	      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(C.line, 0.5); ctx.lineWidth = 0.015 * r;
   197	      const px = x0 + dx, py = y0 + dy, dr = 0.07 * s;
   198	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py - dr * 1.6); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(px + dr, py - dr * 0.2, px + dr * 0.9, py + dr * 0.25);
   199	      ctx.arc(px, py + dr * 0.25, dr * 0.9, 0, Math.PI); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(px - dr, py - dr * 0.2, px, py - dr * 1.6); ctx.closePath();
   200	      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   201	    }
   202	  }
   203	}
   204	
   205	function mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r) {
   206	  const my = G0.mouthY * r;
   207	  const mo = clamp(o.mouthOpen);
   208	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.042 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   209	  ctx.fillStyle = C.mouth;
   210	  switch (o.mouth) {
   211	    case 'o': {
   212	      const rx = (0.055 + 0.11 * mo) * r, ry = (0.04 + 0.16 * mo) * r, cy = my + 0.03 * r * mo;
   213	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   214	      if (mo > 0.55) {
   215	        ctx.save(); ctx.clip();
   216	        ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.25);
   217	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy + ry * 0.75, rx * 0.7, ry * 0.42, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   218	        ctx.restore();
   219	      }
   220	      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r;
   221	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   222	      break;
   223	    }
   224	    case 'smile':
   225	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, my + 0.1 * r, 0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
   226	      break;
   227	    case 'grin': {
   228	      const d = (0.1 + 0.14 * mo) * r;
   229	      const path = () => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.bezierCurveTo(0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.closePath(); };
   230	      path(); ctx.fill();
   231	      ctx.save(); path(); ctx.clip();
   232	      ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.2);
   233	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my + d * 0.7, 0.11 * r, 0.07 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   234	      ctx.restore();
   235	      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r; path(); ctx.stroke();
   236	      break;
   237	    }
   238	    case 'press':
   239	      // straining: a flat, slightly crimped line
   240	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.11 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(-0.03 * r, my - 0.012 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.03 * r, my + 0.008 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.11 * r, my - 0.004 * r); ctx.stroke();
   241	      break;
   242	    case 'none':
   243	      break;
   244	    default:
   245	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.08 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(0.08 * r, my); ctx.stroke();
   246	  }
   247	}

   249	function pacifier(ctx, a, C, r) {
   250	  const my = G0.mouthY * r + 0.02 * r;
   251	  ctx.save();
   252	  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a);
   253	  // the shield over the mouth, a button, and the ring hanging from it
   254	  ctx.lineWidth = 0.032 * r; ctx.strokeStyle = C.line;
   255	  ctx.fillStyle = C.paci || '#7fcbc0';
   256	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my, 0.24 * r, 0.14 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   257	  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.4);
   258	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-0.09 * r, my - 0.055 * r, 0.08 * r, 0.032 * r, -0.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   259	  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   260	  // the ring, hinged on a button in the middle of the shield
   261	  const ry = my + 0.11 * r, rad = 0.11 * r;
   262	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.085 * r;
   263	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, ry, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   264	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.paciRing || '#f6efe2'; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r;
   265	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, ry, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   266	  ctx.fillStyle = mix(C.paci || '#7fcbc0', C.line, 0.3); ctx.strokeStyle = C.line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.02 * r;
   267	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, my, 0.055 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   268	  ctx.restore();
   269	}
   270	
   271	export function drawBaby(ctx, opts) {
   272	  const o = { ...BABY_DEFAULTS, ...opts, free: { ...BABY_DEFAULTS.free, ...(opts.free || {}) } };
   273	  const { r, C } = o;
   274	  const B = G0.body;
   275	  const lw = r * 0.034;
   276	  const s = o.phoneArm === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
   277	  const fs = -s;
   278	  const clay = o.blush > 0 ? mix(C.skin, '#e2574a', 0.1 * clamp(o.blush)) : C.skin;
   279	  const clayDk = C.skinShade, clayLt = C.skinLight || mix(C.skin, '#ffffff', 0.35);

   438	    nub(ctx, root(fs), [fs * 1.62 * r, 0.82 * r], t, t, clay, line, lw);
   439	  } else if (f.mode === 'wave' || f.mode === 'up') {
   440	    const a = f.angle ?? 0;
   441	    const lift = f.mode === 'wave' ? 0.95 + 0.4 * Math.sin(a) : 1.2 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a);
   442	    const len = 0.62 * r;
   443	    nub(ctx, root(fs), [fs * (arm.x * r + Math.cos(lift) * len), arm.y * r - Math.sin(lift) * len], t, t * 1.05, clay, line, lw);
   444	  } else if (f.mode === 'point') {
   445	    // the nub coming at the lens: it grows, its flat end toward us
   446	    const k = clamp(f.reach ?? 1);
   447	    const hx = lerp(fs * 1.5, fs * 0.3, k) * r, hy = lerp(0.9, 1.0, k) * r, hr = lerp(0.2, 0.7, k) * r;
   448	    nub(ctx, root(fs), [hx, hy], t, hr * 1.6, clay, line, lw);
   449	    rr(ctx, hx - hr, hy - hr * 0.9, 2 * hr, 1.8 * hr, hr * 0.45);
   450	    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.08); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
   451	  } else if (f.mode === 'hold') {
   452	    // the nub reaches the point the scene gives (head-relative units); the scene draws the prop
   453	    const hx = (f.at?.[0] ?? fs * 0.6) * r, hy = (f.at?.[1] ?? 1.2) * r;
   454	    nub(ctx, root(fs), [hx, hy], t, t * 1.15, clay, line, lw);
   455	  }
   456	  ctx.restore();
   457	}

    16	/** Wall with a soft light falloff (brighter toward the window side). */
    17	export function wall(ctx, G, { lightX = 200, lightY = 300, glow = 0.18 } = {}) {
    18	  fillAll(ctx, G.wall);
    19	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(lightX, lightY, 50, lightX, lightY, 1500);

    22	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
    23	  ctx.fillRect(BIG.x, BIG.y, BIG.w, BIG.h);
    24	}

    26	/** A window with curtains; the sky colour follows the time of day. */
    27	export function windowPane(ctx, G, { x = 70, y = 230, w = 360, h = 520, curtains = true, blinds = 0 } = {}) {
    28	  ctx.save();
    29	  ctx.fillStyle = G.trim;
    30	  ctx.fillRect(x - 22, y - 22, w + 44, h + 44);
    31	  const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, y + h);
    32	  sky.addColorStop(0, G.sky);
    33	  sky.addColorStop(1, mix(G.sky, G.window, 0.7));
    34	  ctx.fillStyle = sky;
    35	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    36	  if (blinds > 0) {

    38	    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) ctx.fillRect(x, y + i * (h / 14), w, (h / 14) * blinds * 0.55);
    39	  }

    41	  ctx.fillRect(x + w / 2 - 8, y, 16, h);
    42	  ctx.fillRect(x, y + h * 0.45 - 8, w, 16);
    43	  if (curtains) {

    45	    for (const [cx, dir] of [[x - 60, 1], [x + w + 60, -1]]) {

    51	      ctx.closePath();
    52	      ctx.fill();
    53	    }
    54	  }

    56	}

    78	/** Doorway on the far wall; `open` 0..1 widens the lit gap. Returns the light rect. */
    79	export function doorway(ctx, G, { x = 690, y = 120, w = 300, h = 760, open = 1, hall = '#ffcf8a', hallAlpha = 1 } = {}) {
    80	  ctx.save();
    81	  ctx.fillStyle = G.trim;
    82	  ctx.fillRect(x - 24, y - 24, w + 48, h + 24);
    83	  const gw = w * clamp(open);
    84	  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + gw, 0);
    85	  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(hall, 0.95 * hallAlpha));
    86	  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(mix(hall, '#ffffff', 0.3), 0.85 * hallAlpha));
    87	  ctx.fillStyle = G.doorDark;
    88	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    89	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
    90	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, gw, h);
    91	  // the door leaf, swung open toward the room
    92	  ctx.fillStyle = G.door;
    93	  ctx.beginPath();
    94	  ctx.moveTo(x + gw, y);
    95	  ctx.lineTo(x + w + 10, y - 20);
    96	  ctx.lineTo(x + w + 10, y + h + 30);
    97	  ctx.lineTo(x + gw, y + h);
    98	  ctx.closePath();
    99	  ctx.fill();
   100	  ctx.restore();
   101	  return { x, y, w: gw, h };
   102	}

   104	/** Light spill on the floor/wall from an open door at night. */
   105	export function spill(ctx, x, y, w, h, color, a = 0.25) {
   106	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x + w / 2, y + h, 20, x + w / 2, y + h, h * 1.1);

   113	/**
   114	 * Adult Clawd (the parent): the classic proportions, a 10u x 6u clay block on four legs with
   115	 * two arm nubs and tall slit eyes. (x, y) = the ground point between the feet; u = unit.
   116	 *   pose 'stand' | 'sit' (on the floor, legs out toward us) | 'slump' (in a chair, tipped back)
   117	 *   eyes 'open' | 'tired' (heavy lids) | 'closed' | null (a pure silhouette)
   118	 *   mouth 'o' (asleep, breathing: breath 0..1) | 'line' | null
   119	 * color fills everything when there is no `clay` (a night silhouette); rim = a rim-light colour.
   120	 */
   121	export function adultClawd(ctx, { x, y, u, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', clay = null, clayDk = null, ink = '#1e1412', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = null, lean = 0, mouth = null, breath = 0.5, arms = [0.25, 0.25] }) {
   122	  ctx.save();
   123	  ctx.translate(x, y);
   124	  ctx.rotate(lean);
   125	  const fill = clay || color, dk = clayDk || (clay ? mix(clay, '#000000', 0.25) : color);
   126	  const lw = clay ? Math.max(2, u * 0.16) : 0;
   127	  const stroke = () => { if (lw) { ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke(); } };
   128	  const legH = pose === 'stand' ? 2.2 : 0;
   129	  const top = -(6 + legH) * u, bot = -legH * u;
   130	  // arm nubs (behind the body), raise angles in radians
   131	  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
   132	    const a = arms[k < 0 ? 0 : 1];
   133	    ctx.save();
   134	    ctx.translate(k * 4.9 * u, top + 3.5 * u);
   135	    ctx.rotate(k < 0 ? a : -a);
   136	    rrect(ctx, k < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u);
   137	    ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); stroke();
   138	    ctx.restore();
   139	  }
   140	  // legs: standing under the body; sitting, the feet point at us below the front edge
   141	  ctx.fillStyle = dk;
   142	  for (const lx of [-4, -2, 1, 3]) {
   143	    if (pose === 'stand') { rrect(ctx, lx * u, -2.4 * u, u, 2.4 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
   144	    else { rrect(ctx, (lx - 0.1) * u, bot - 0.5 * u, 1.2 * u, 1.1 * u, 0.3 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
   145	  }
   146	  // body
   147	  rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u);
   148	  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
   149	  if (clay) {
   150	    ctx.save(); rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u); ctx.clip();
   151	    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(dk, 0.5); ctx.fillRect(-5 * u, top + 4.2 * u, 10 * u, 2 * u);
   152	    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.35), 0.35);
   153	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-1.6 * u, top + 1.6 * u, 3.4 * u, 1.3 * u, -0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   154	    ctx.restore();
   155	    rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u); stroke();
   156	  }
   157	  if (rim) {
   158	    ctx.strokeStyle = rim; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.3; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   159	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-4.6 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.7 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.95 * u, top + 2.5 * u); ctx.stroke();
   160	  }
   161	  // face: eyes at (+-2.5u, 2u below the top), mouth 3.7u below the top
   162	  const ec = eyeColor || ink;
   163	  if (eyes) {
   164	    for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
   165	      const ex = k * 2.5 * u, ey = top + 2 * u;
   166	      ctx.fillStyle = ec; ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   167	      if (eyes === 'closed') {
   168	        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.32;
   169	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.8 * u, ex + 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.stroke();
   170	      } else if (eyes === 'tired') {
   171	        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey + 0.05 * u, u, 0.95 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
   172	        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.24;
   173	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.85 * u, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + 0.85 * u, ey + k * 0.12 * u); ctx.stroke();
   174	      } else {
   175	        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey - u, u, 2 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
   176	      }
   177	    }
   178	  }
   179	  if (mouth === 'o') {
   180	    const o = 0.75 + 0.25 * breath;
   181	    ctx.fillStyle = clay ? '#4a1f2a' : rgba('#000000', 0.55);
   182	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, top + 3.9 * u, 0.6 * u, 0.85 * u * o, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   183	    if (lw) { ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.8; ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.stroke(); }
   184	  } else if (mouth === 'line') {
   185	    ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.25;
   186	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.lineTo(0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.stroke();
   187	  }
   188	  ctx.restore();
   189	}

   196	export function parent(ctx, { x, y, u = 30, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', clay = null, clayDk = null, ink = '#0d0d12', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = '#c9ccd1', lean = 0, mouth = null }) {
   197	  adultClawd(ctx, { x, y, u, pose, color, clay, clayDk, ink, rim, eyes, eyeColor, lean, mouth, arms: pose === 'stand' ? [-0.5, -0.5] : [-0.9, -0.7] });

   200	/** Ceiling fan seen from below. angle in radians; blur 0..1 adds ghost blades. */
   201	export function ceilingFan(ctx, G, { x = 540, y = 820, R = 520, angle = 0, blur = 0, blades = 5 } = {}) {
   202	  ctx.save();
   203	  ctx.translate(x, y);
   204	  const ghosts = blur > 0.05 ? 4 : 0;
   205	  for (let gI = ghosts; gI >= 0; gI--) {
   206	    ctx.globalAlpha = gI === 0 ? 1 : 0.18 * blur;
   207	    for (let i = 0; i < blades; i++) {
   208	      const a = angle - gI * 0.09 * blur + (i * TAU) / blades;
   209	      ctx.save();
   210	      ctx.rotate(a);
   211	      ctx.fillStyle = G.fan;
   212	      rrect(ctx, 70, -58, R - 70, 116, 50); ctx.fill();
   213	      ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.fanShade, 0.8);
   214	      rrect(ctx, 70, 18, R - 70, 40, 20); ctx.fill();
   215	      ctx.fillStyle = G.metal;
   216	      ctx.fillRect(40, -16, 90, 32);
   217	      ctx.restore();
   218	    }
   219	  }
   220	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   221	  ctx.fillStyle = G.metal;
   222	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 92, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   223	  ctx.fillStyle = G.lamp;
   224	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 60, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   225	  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.35);
   226	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-14, -16, 22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   227	  ctx.restore();
   228	}

   236	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
   237	  ctx.fillRect(BIG.x, BIG.y, BIG.w, BIG.h);
   238	  ceilingFan(ctx, G, fan);
   239	}

   287	export function foodBlob(ctx, color, x, y, r, seed = 0) {
   288	  ctx.fillStyle = color;
   289	  ctx.beginPath();
   290	  const n = 9;
   291	  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
   292	    const a = (i / n) * TAU;
   293	    const rr = r * (0.82 + 0.3 * hash(i + seed * 17, 5));
   294	    const px = x + Math.cos(a) * rr, py = y + Math.sin(a) * rr;
   295	    i ? ctx.lineTo(px, py) : ctx.moveTo(px, py);
   296	  }
   297	  ctx.closePath();
   298	  ctx.fill();
   299	}

   301	/** A splat stuck on the lens: big, soft-edged, slightly translucent. */
   302	export function lensSplat(ctx, color, x, y, r, k = 1, seed = 3) {
   303	  ctx.save();
   304	  ctx.globalAlpha *= 0.92;
   305	  foodBlob(ctx, color, x, y, r * k, seed);
   306	  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
   307	    const a = hash(i, seed + 40) * TAU, d = r * (1.1 + 0.6 * hash(i, seed + 41)) * k;
   308	    foodBlob(ctx, color, x + Math.cos(a) * d, y + Math.sin(a) * d, r * 0.16 * (0.6 + hash(i, seed + 42)) * k, seed + i);
   309	  }
   310	  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.25);
   311	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(x - r * 0.3 * k, y - r * 0.35 * k, r * 0.28 * k, r * 0.16 * k, -0.5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   312	  ctx.restore();
   313	}

   315	/** Water droplets on the lens (bath). */
   316	export function droplets(ctx, n, seed, t, { alpha = 1 } = {}) {
   317	  ctx.save();
   318	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   319	    const x = hash(i, seed) * CW, y0 = hash(i, seed + 1) * CH;
   320	    const r = 14 + 46 * Math.pow(hash(i, seed + 2), 2);
   321	    const y = y0 + Math.max(0, t - hash(i, seed + 3) * 1.2) * 60 * (r / 40);
   322	    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * 0.9;
   323	    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r * 0.3, y - r * 0.3, r * 0.1, x, y, r);
   324	    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)');
   325	    g.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)');
   326	    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.32)');
   327	    ctx.fillStyle = g;
   328	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   329	  }
   330	  ctx.restore();
   331	}

   449	/** Stopwatch overlay badge (6:15 "still standing"). */
   450	export function timerBadge(ctx, secs, { x = 540, y = 1260 } = {}) {
   451	  const m = Math.floor(secs / 60), s = Math.floor(secs % 60), cs = Math.floor((secs % 1) * 100);
   452	  const txt = `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(cs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   453	  ctx.save();
   454	  ctx.font = '700 92px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
   455	  const w = ctx.measureText(txt).width;
   456	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
   457	  rrect(ctx, x - w / 2 - 110, y - 80, w + 150, 120, 60); ctx.fill();
   458	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
   459	  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   460	  ctx.fillText(txt, x - w / 2 + 20, y + 10);
   461	  // stopwatch glyph
   462	  ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.lineWidth = 9;
   463	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x - w / 2 - 50, y - 18, 30, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   464	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - w / 2 - 50, y - 18); ctx.lineTo(x - w / 2 - 50 + 16 * Math.sin(secs * TAU / 4), y - 18 - 16 * Math.cos(secs * TAU / 4)); ctx.stroke();
   465	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.fillRect(x - w / 2 - 60, y - 64, 20, 14);
   466	  ctx.restore();
   467	}

    13	/** Baby colours for this clip: the flash palette at night, the graded room light by day. */
    14	export function babyLight(C, outfit, mode = null) {
    15	  const m = mode || (C.G.light.night > 0.5 ? 'flash' : 'ambient');
    16	  if (m === 'flash') return babyColors(FLASH, outfit);
    17	  // daylight: the baby takes about half the room's colour cast
    18	  return babyColors(C.G.light.night > 0.5 ? C.G : grade(C.G.light.min, undefined, 0.5), outfit);

    21	/** Night flash: everything away from the face falls off into the dark. */
    22	export function flashFalloff(ctx, x, y, r, strength = 0.85) {
    23	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, r * 1.05, x, y, r * 3.4);
    24	  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(2,3,10,0)');
    25	  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(2,3,10,${strength})`);
    26	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
    27	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    28	}

    30	/** A quick white pop when the flash fires on a cut. */
    31	export function flashPop(ctx, lt, a = 0.55) {
    32	  if (lt < 0) return;
    33	  const k = Math.exp(-lt / 0.07) * a;
    34	  if (k < 0.01) return;
    35	  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', k);
    36	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    37	}

    39	/** Word start time inside the clip's caption words (by text), or a fallback. */
    40	export function wordT(C, match, fallback = C.start, nth = 0) {
    41	  const m = String(match).toLowerCase();
    42	  let n = nth;
    43	  for (const w of C.words || []) {
    44	    const s = String(w.w).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9:']/g, '');
    45	    if (s === m || s.includes(m)) { if (n-- === 0) return w.start; }
    46	  }
    47	  return fallback;
    48	}

    50	/** Index of the beat (within the clip) and its phase. */
    51	export function clipBeat(C) {
    52	  const b0 = C.B.beatIndex(C.start), b = C.B.beatIndex(C.t);
    53	  return { i: Math.floor(b - b0 + 1e-6), phase: (b - b0) - Math.floor(b - b0 + 1e-6) };
    54	}
     1	// Generic daytime selfie (also the fallback for any clause without a rule): the nursery
     2	// wall and window behind, the baby close to the lens, plus an optional prop.
     3	//   prop: 'bottle' | 'book' (bite) | 'point'      mode: 'fight' | 'cry'      crib: true
     4	import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
     5	import { wall, windowPane, cribBack, bottle, boardBook } from '../props.js';
     6	import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, flashFalloff, clipBeat, wordT } from './common.js';
     7	import { rrect } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
     8	
     9	export function room(ctx, C, { crib = false } = {}) {
    10	  const { G } = C;
    11	  wall(ctx, G);
    12	  windowPane(ctx, G, { x: 60, y: 260, w: 330, h: 470 });
    13	  if (crib) cribBack(ctx, G);
    14	}
    15	
    16	export default {
    17	  camera(C) { return C.params.prop === 'point' ? { shake: 1.6 } : {}; },
    18	  draw(ctx, C) {
    19	    const p = C.params;
    20	    const { G } = C;
    21	    room(ctx, C, { crib: !!p.crib });
    22	    const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie');
    23	    const o = { ...SELFIE, C: L, outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'open', mouth: 'line' };
    24	    const cb = clipBeat(C);
    25	    if (p.mode === 'fight') {
    26	      // eyes close slowly, snap open on the beat, twice
    27	      const half = (cb.i % 2) + cb.phase;          // 0..2 within each two-beat cycle
    28	      const cycle = Math.floor(cb.i / 2);
    29	      o.open = cycle < 2 ? 1 - ease.inCubic(clamp(half / 1.75)) : 0.15;
    30	      o.eyes = 'open'; o.mouth = 'press';
    31	      o.tilt = 0.06 * Math.sin(half * 0.8);
    32	    } else if (p.mode === 'cry') {
    33	      o.eyes = 'squeeze'; o.mouth = 'o'; o.mouthOpen = 0.9 + 0.1 * Math.sin(C.t * 18); o.tears = (C.lt * 0.8) % 1; o.blush = 0.5;
    34	    } else if (p.prop === 'point') {
    35	      o.free = { mode: 'none' };
    36	      o.mouth = 'o'; o.mouthOpen = 0.45; o.look = [0, 0.4];
    37	    } else if (p.prop === 'bottle') {
    38	      o.eyes = 'half'; o.open = 0.45; o.mouth = 'none';
    39	      o.free = { mode: 'hold', at: [0.62, 1.32] };
    40	    } else if (p.prop === 'book') {
    41	      o.eyes = 'open'; o.look = p.bite ? [0, 0.2] : [0, -0.5]; o.mouth = p.bite ? 'o' : 'line'; o.mouthOpen = 0.7;
    42	      o.free = { mode: 'hold', at: [0.55, 1.35] };
    43	    }
    44	    drawBaby(ctx, o);
    45	    if (p.prop === 'bottle') {
    46	      // nipple tip at the mouth, the bottle tipped up toward the lens
    47	      const sip = 0.04 * Math.sin(C.t * 9);
    48	      bottle(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x + 95, y: SELFIE.y + 268, a: -0.62 + sip, s: 1.05, level: 0.55 - 0.15 * C.lt / C.dur });
    49	    } else if (p.prop === 'book') {
    50	      // the book's corner in the mouth (corner solved to land on the mouth)
    51	      if (p.bite) boardBook(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x + 330, y: SELFIE.y + 120 + 6 * Math.sin(C.t * 10), a: -0.75, s: 0.9 });
    52	      else {
    53	        const lift = ease.outBack(seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + 0.3));
    54	        boardBook(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x + 40, y: SELFIE.y + 520 - 140 * lift, a: -0.08, s: 1.0 });
    55	      }
    56	    }
    57	    if (G.light.night > 0.5) flashFalloff(ctx, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r);
    58	  },
    59	  overlay(ctx, C) {
    60	    if (C.params.prop !== 'point') return;
    61	    // the nub coming straight at the lens: huge, soft, out of focus, its square end toward us
    62	    const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + 0.05, C.start + Math.min(0.5, C.dur * 0.55)));
    63	    const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie');
    64	    const x = 640 - 30 * k, y = 2050 - 560 * k, r = 90 + 180 * k;
    65	    ctx.save();
    66	    ctx.filter = `blur(${(6 + 22 * k).toFixed(1)}px)`;
    67	    ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(-0.1);
    68	    ctx.fillStyle = L.skin;
    69	    rrect(ctx, -r, -r * 0.85, 2 * r, r * 5, r * 0.42); ctx.fill();
    70	    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(L.skinShade, 0.55);
    71	    rrect(ctx, -r * 0.92, r * 0.55, r * 1.84, r * 0.3, r * 0.15); ctx.fill();
    72	    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(L.skinLight || '#ffffff', 0.45);
    73	    rrect(ctx, -r * 0.7, -r * 0.65, r * 1.0, r * 0.4, r * 0.2); ctx.fill();
    74	    ctx.restore();
    75	  },
    76	};
     1	// Crib at night. Chorus: 2am / 3am / 4am crying, the same framing with the parent
     2	// silhouette progressing (absent -> in the lit doorway -> sitting on the floor by the crib).
     3	// Verse 2 modes: quiet (5am: nearly black, two open eyes), fake (7:45 "sleeping": eyes wide,
     4	// a cut-in of the parents' IR monitor view), sleep (8pm-12am: eyes closed, the phone
     5	// drifting, darker and stiller), void (1am: black, record dot, the eyes open in the silence).
     6	import { drawBaby, babyColors, BABY_GEOM } from '../baby.js';
     7	import { rrect } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
     8	import { wall, cribBack, doorway, parent, spill, fillAll } from '../props.js';
     9	import { FLASH, grade } from '../tod.js';
    10	import { drawMonitorCutIn } from '../monitor.js';
    11	import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, flashFalloff, flashPop, CW, CH } from './common.js';
    12	
    13	const HALL = '#ffc97e';
    14	
    15	function nightRoom(ctx, C, { door = 0, sit = 0, stand = 0 }) {
    16	  const { G } = C;
    17	  wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0 });
    18	  if (door > 0) {
    19	    const d = doorway(ctx, G, { x: 700, y: 150, w: 300, h: 720, open: 0.75 * door, hall: HALL, hallAlpha: door });
    20	    spill(ctx, d.x, d.y + 300, d.w, 600, HALL, 0.18 * door);
    21	    if (stand > 0) {
    22	      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = stand;
    23	      // down the lit hall, small against the light, above the caption
    24	      parent(ctx, { x: 818, y: 440, u: 20, pose: 'stand', color: '#0d0e14', rim: rgba(HALL, 0.55) });
    25	      ctx.restore();
    26	    }
    27	  }
    28	  if (sit > 0) {
    29	    // on the floor by the crib, in the hallway light's spill, eyes half shut
    30	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = sit;
    31	    spill(ctx, 640, 700, 420, 700, HALL, 0.3);
    32	    // slumped against the crib, the tired eyes just over the baby's shoulder
    33	    parent(ctx, { x: 925, y: 1010, u: 56, pose: 'sit', clay: '#7a4a3e', clayDk: '#55332c', ink: '#0d0d12', rim: rgba(HALL, 0.85), eyes: 'tired', eyeColor: rgba('#f0e2cc', 0.95), lean: -0.14 });
    34	    ctx.restore();
    35	  }
    36	  cribBack(ctx, G, { railY: 700, bottomY: 1500 });
    37	}
    38	
    39	export default {
    40	  camera(C) {
    41	    const p = C.params;
    42	    if (p.mode === 'void' || (p.mode === 'sleep' && (p.dark || 0) >= 2)) return { still: true, grain: 1.4 };
    43	    if (p.mode === 'sleep') return { shake: 0.5, kick: 0.3, tilt: 0.05 * Math.sin(C.lt * 0.9) + 0.03, zoom: 1 + 0.03 * C.lt };
    44	    if (p.mode === 'quiet') return { shake: 0.3, kick: 0.2, grain: 1.5 };
    45	    if (p.stage === 1) return { tilt: 0.26 };
    46	    if (p.stage === 2) return { tilt: -0.05 };
    47	    return {};
    48	  },
    49	  caption(C) {
    50	    const p = C.params;
    51	    if (p.stage != null) return { slam: ['crying'] };
    52	    return {};
    53	  },
    54	  draw(ctx, C) {
    55	    const p = C.params;
    56	    const { G, lt } = C;
    57	    if (p.mode === 'void' || p.mode === 'quiet') {
    58	      fillAll(ctx, p.mode === 'void' ? '#000000' : '#04050a');
    59	      // two open eyes in the dark
    60	      const eyesAt = p.mode === 'void' ? C.B.snap(C.end - C.B.barDur, 'downbeat') : C.start + 0.12;
    61	      const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, eyesAt, eyesAt + 0.25));
    62	      if (k > 0) {
    63	        if (p.mode === 'quiet') {
    64	          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.07;
    65	          drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(G, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'wide', mouth: 'none', pacifier: 1 });
    66	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    67	        }
    68	        // Clawd's eyes (tall rounded rectangles, the highlight up and left), lit by the screen
    69	        const E = BABY_GEOM.eye, R = SELFIE.r;
    70	        const w = E.w * R * 0.72, h = E.h * R * 0.72;
    71	        for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    72	          const ex = SELFIE.x + s * E.dx * R, ey = SELFIE.y + E.dy * R;
    73	          const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(ex, ey, 2, ex, ey, h * 1.25);
    74	          g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#e8eeff', 0.85 * k));
    75	          g.addColorStop(0.4, rgba('#c9d4f0', 0.3 * k));
    76	          g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#c9d4f0', 0));
    77	          ctx.fillStyle = g;
    78	          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey, h * 1.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    79	          ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#05060c', k);
    80	          rrect(ctx, ex - w / 2, ey - h / 2, w, h, w * 0.4); ctx.fill();
    81	          ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', k);
    82	          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex - w * 0.17, ey - h * 0.23, w * 0.26, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    83	        }
    84	      }
    85	      return;
    86	    }
    87	    if (p.mode === 'sleep') {
    88	      const dark = p.dark || 0;
    89	      nightRoom(ctx, C, {});
    90	      // a dim night-light: the face just readable in the dark
    91	      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(grade(Math.max(C.min, 1200), undefined, 0.55), 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line', pacifier: 1, free: { mode: 'down' } });
    92	      // a dim night-light glow on the face, dimmer each hour
    93	      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(SELFIE.x - 200, SELFIE.y - 100, 40, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r * 2.2);
    94	      g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ff5a3c', 0.16 / (1 + dark)));
    95	      g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ff5a3c', 0));
    96	      ctx.fillStyle = g;
    97	      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    98	      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000006', 0.05 + 0.2 * dark);
    99	      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
   100	      return;
   101	    }
   102	    if (p.mode === 'fake') {
   103	      nightRoom(ctx, C, {});
   104	      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(FLASH, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'wide', mouth: 'line', look: [0, 0], flash: 0.6, pacifier: 1 });
   105	      flashFalloff(ctx, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r, 0.9);
   106	      flashPop(ctx, lt, 0.4);
   107	      // the parents' monitor view cut in for the second half of the clip
   108	      const cutA = C.start + C.dur * 0.5;
   109	      if (C.t >= cutA) drawMonitorCutIn(ctx, C, C.t - cutA);
   110	      return;
   111	    }
   112	    // chorus crying: stage 0 / 1 / 2
   113	    const st = p.stage || 0;
   114	    const appear = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.15, C.start + C.dur * 0.6));
   115	    nightRoom(ctx, C, { door: st >= 1 ? 1 : 0, stand: st === 1 ? appear : 0, sit: st === 2 ? 1 : 0 });
   116	    drawBaby(ctx, {
   117	      ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(FLASH, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'squeeze', mouth: 'o',
   118	      mouthOpen: 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(C.t * 21), tears: (lt * 0.9) % 1, flash: 0.75, blush: 0.35,
   119	      free: { mode: 'down' },
   120	    });
   121	    flashFalloff(ctx, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r, 0.78);
   122	    flashPop(ctx, lt, 0.5);
   123	  },
   124	};

     2	// shared renderer. Every frame: map the outro onto the intro (the loop), build a clip

     4	// lens overlays -> caption -> status bar), then lay it out: full frame (9:16) or inside a

    42	/** Words the caption shows at t for this window. */
    43	function captionWords(env, w, t) {
    44	  const st = env.story;
    45	  if (w.plate === 'hello') return w.lines.flatMap((l) => l.words);
    46	  const c = st.clauseAt(t, w);
    47	  return c ? c.words : [];
    48	}
    49	
    50	/** Clip descriptor: everything a clip type needs, nothing read from global state. */
    51	export function makeClip(env, t, w, extra = {}) {
    52	  const st = env.story;
    53	  const min = extra.min ?? st.clock(t);
    54	  const G = grade(min);
    55	  return {
    56	    type: w.plate, params: w.params, win: w, t, lt: t - w.start, dur: w.dur, start: w.start, end: w.end,
    57	    min, G, night: G.light.night > 0.5, words: captionWords(env, w, t), clause: st.clauseAt(t, w),
    58	    B: env.B, L: env.L, env, fps: env.fps, story: st, tile: false, ...extra,
    59	  };
    60	}

    62	/** Draw one camera frame (1080x1920 camera space) for clip C. */
    63	export function drawCameraFrame(ctx, C, { chrome = true } = {}) {
    64	  const scene = scenes[C.type];
    65	  const cam = scene.camera?.(C) || {};
    66	  ctx.save();
    67	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, CW, CH); ctx.clip();
    68	  ctx.fillStyle = C.G.wall;
    69	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
    70	  ctx.save();
    71	  cameraTransform(ctx, C, C.tile ? { ...cam, still: true } : cam);
    72	  scene.draw(ctx, C);
    73	  ctx.restore();
    74	  lensPost(ctx, C, cam);
    75	  if (scene.overlay) { ctx.save(); scene.overlay(ctx, C); ctx.restore(); }
    76	  if (chrome) {
    77	    const capOpts = scene.caption ? scene.caption(C) : {};
    78	    if (capOpts !== false) drawCaption(ctx, C.t, C.words, { ...CAPTION, ...capOpts });
    79	    const st = C.story;
    80	    drawStatusBar(ctx, { min: C.min, battery: st.battery(C.t), rec: st.rec(C.t) });
    81	  }
    82	  ctx.restore();
    83	}
