    88	function drawBackdrop(ctx, C) {
    89	  const G = C.G;
    90	  const W = 1920, H = 1080;
    91	  ctx.fillStyle = G.wall;
    92	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    93	  const night = G.light.night;
    94	  // window light (left), soft
    95	  const wx = 330, wy = 380;
    96	  let g = ctx.createRadialGradient(wx, wy, 40, wx, wy, 520);
    97	  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(mix(G.window, '#ffffff', 0.3), 0.85 * (1 - night) + 0.1));
    98	  g.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.window, 0.35 * (1 - night) + 0.04));
    99	  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.window, 0));
   100	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
   101	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   102	  // crib slats (right), out of focus
   103	  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
   104	    const x = 1360 + i * 62;
   105	    const sg = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - 26, 0, x + 26, 0);
   106	    sg.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.crib, 0));
   107	    sg.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.crib, 0.55));
   108	    sg.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.crib, 0));
   109	    ctx.fillStyle = sg;
   110	    ctx.fillRect(x - 26, 470, 52, 520);
   111	  }
   112	  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.crib, 0.4);
   113	  ctx.fillRect(1310, 440, 600, 40);
   114	  // floor
   115	  g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 900, 0, H);
   116	  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.floor, 0));
   117	  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.floor, 0.9));
   118	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
   119	  ctx.fillRect(0, 900, W, 180);
   120	  // a lamp bokeh at night
   121	  if (night > 0.3) {
   122	    for (const [bx, by, br] of [[260, 760, 70], [330, 700, 40], [1700, 300, 55]]) {
   123	      const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(bx, by, 0, bx, by, br);
   124	      bg.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0.35 * night));
   125	      bg.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0));
   126	      ctx.fillStyle = bg;
   127	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bx, by, br, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   128	    }
   129	  }
   130	  // the sun / moon arc (top right)
   131	  const ax = 1660, ay = 230, ar = 150;
   132	  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(night > 0.5 ? '#c9d3ff' : mix(G.wall, '#000000', 0.45), 0.55);
   133	  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   134	  ctx.setLineDash([4, 12]);
   135	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ax, ay, ar, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   136	  ctx.setLineDash([]);
   137	  const sb = skyBody(C.min);
   138	  const a = Math.PI + sb.p * Math.PI;
   139	  const px = ax + Math.cos(a) * ar, py = ay + Math.sin(a) * ar;
   140	  ctx.fillStyle = sb.body === 'sun' ? '#ffd36b' : '#e8ecff';
   141	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   142	  if (sb.body === 'moon') { ctx.fillStyle = G.wall; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px + 8, py - 6, 15, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   143	  // soft vignette
   144	  g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 300, W / 2, H / 2, 1150);
   145	  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
   146	  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${0.35 + 0.3 * night})`);
   147	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
   148	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   149	}

   151	function drawPhone(ctx, C) {
   152	  const p = PHONE;
   153	  // shadow
   154	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sw * 0.3, p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sh * 0.75);

   159	  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d10';

   168	  ctx.translate(p.sx, p.sy);
   169	  ctx.scale(p.sw / CW, p.sh / CH);
   170	  drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
   171	  ctx.restore();
   172	}
   173	
   174	export default {
   175	  id: '2am-crying',
   176	  layouts: { '9x16': { w: 1080, h: 1920 }, '16x9': { w: 1920, h: 1080 } },
   177	  defaultLayout: '9x16',
   178	  fonts: FONTS,
   179	  timeline,
   180	  sectionPlates: SECTION_PLATES,   // render.mjs reports these at start-up (MISSING if absent)
   181	  scenes,
   182	  audioCandidates(audio) {
   183	    const synth = ['../synth/out/2am_crying.wav'];
   184	    const click = ['audio/click.wav'];
   185	    // a real take names its file in the source: "suno runE2 13e92be2 (../audio/runE2/<file>.mp3)"
   186	    const named = /\(([^()]+\.(?:mp3|wav|m4a))\)/.exec(String(audio.source || ''));
   187	    if (named) return [named[1], ...click];
   188	    return String(audio.source || '').startsWith('synth') ? [...synth, ...click] : [...click];
   189	  },
   190	  setup(env) {
   191	    env.story = makeStory(env);
   192	    env.drawCameraFrame = drawCameraFrame;   // the grid draws other clips as tiles
   193	  },
   194	  /** The song time whose frame an outro frame repeats (render.mjs --check compares the two). */
   195	  loopSource(env, t) { return env.story.loopTime(t); },
   196	  /** Extra contact-sheet cells: stages of the long take and the grid filling in. */
   197	  sheetCells(env) {
   198	    const out = [];
   199	    for (const w of env.T.windows) {
   200	      if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'long') for (const f of [0.3, 0.72, 0.9]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `long ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
   201	      if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'tip') out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * 0.68, label: 'tip 68%' });
   202	      if (w.plate === 'grid') for (const f of [0.2, 0.97]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `grid ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
   203	    }
   204	    return out;
   205	  },
   206	  frame(ctx, t, env) {
   207	    const st = env.story;
   208	    const tm = st.loopTime(t);
   209	    const w = st.windowFor(tm);
   210	    const C = makeClip(env, tm, w);
   211	    if (env.layout === '16x9') {
   212	      drawBackdrop(ctx, C);
   213	      drawPhone(ctx, C);
   214	    } else {
   215	      drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
   216	    }
   217	  },

     1	// Song-level curves for v3 "the day", all derived from lyric content:
     2	//   loopTime(t)  outro frames map frame-exactly onto intro frames (the video loops)
     3	//   clock(t)     status-bar clock, minutes since midnight (the escalating number)
     4	//   battery(t)   drains across the day, charges during naps
     5	//   rec(t)       record dot + running timer (starts with the beat in the intro)
     6	//   clause(t)    the time clause on screen (caption words, minutes)
     7	
     8	import { clamp, lerp, bisect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
     9	import { clausesOf } from './clips.js';
    10	
    11	const NAP_TYPES = new Set(['nap']);
    12	
    13	export function makeStory(env) {
    14	  const { L, B, T } = env;
    15	  const clauses = clausesOf(L);
    16	  const W = T.windows;
    17	  const intro = W[0];
    18	  const outro = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'hello' && w.params.loop) || null;
    19	
    20	  // The outro is the intro again, frame-exactly: outro frame k shows intro frame k - shift
    21	  // (same t -> same pixels, every input included). The shift lines the outro's "Hi" up with
    22	  // the intro's: a real vocal does not start them at the same offset into their windows
    23	  // (Suno runE2: 2.80 s into the intro, 0.18 s into the outro), and the caption is the
    24	  // lyric. It is a whole number of frames, so every outro frame is an intro frame.
    25	  const fps = env.fps || 30;
    26	  const hiIntro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'intro' });
    27	  const hiOutro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
    28	  const shiftFrames = outro
    29	    ? Math.round(((hiIntro && hiOutro) ? hiOutro.start - hiIntro.start : outro.start - intro.start) * fps)
    30	    : 0;
    31	  function loopTime(t) {
    32	    if (!outro || t < outro.start) return t;
    33	    return Math.max(intro.start, (Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames) / fps);
    34	  }
    35	  const windowFor = (t) => (outro && t >= outro.start ? intro : T.windowAt(t));
    36	
    37	  // ---- which clause is on screen ---------------------------------------------------
    38	  function clauseAt(t, w = windowFor(t)) {
    39	    const p = w.params;
    40	    if (p.clause != null) return clauses[p.clause];
    41	    if (p.clauses) {
    42	      const list = p.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
    43	      let cur = list[0];
    44	      for (const c of list) if (B.snap(c.start, 'downbeat') <= t + 1e-6 || c.start <= t) cur = c;
    45	      return cur;
    46	    }
    47	    return null;
    48	  }
    49	
    50	  // ---- clock -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	  const INTRO_MIN = 119; // 1:59am: the phone wakes a minute before the first clip
    52	  function clock(t) {
    53	    const w = windowFor(t);
    54	    if (w.plate === 'hello') return INTRO_MIN;
    55	    if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'long') {
    56	      // the one long take ticks minute by minute between the sung times
    57	      const list = w.params.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
    58	      const i = bisect(list, t, (c) => c.start);
    59	      if (i < 0) return list[0].min;
    60	      const a = list[i], b = list[i + 1];
    61	      if (!b) return a.min + Math.floor(clamp((t - a.start) / (w.end - a.start)) * 1.99);
    62	      return Math.floor(lerp(a.min, b.min, clamp((t - a.start) / (b.start - a.start))));
    63	    }
    64	    const c = clauseAt(t, w);
    65	    return c ? c.min : INTRO_MIN;
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  // ---- battery: integrate drain / charge over the windows ------------------------------
    69	  const DRAIN = 1.8, CHARGE = 2.0; // percent per second: ends the day in the red
    70	  const isNap = (w) => NAP_TYPES.has(w.plate);
    71	  const batt0 = [];
    72	  let lvl = 100;
    73	  for (const w of W) {
    74	    batt0.push(lvl);
    75	    if (w === outro) break;
    76	    lvl = clamp(lvl + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * w.dur, 4, 100);
    77	  }
    78	  function battery(t) {
    79	    const w = windowFor(t);
    80	    const i = W.indexOf(w);
    81	    const v = batt0[i] + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * (t - w.start);
    82	    return { level: clamp(v, 4, 100) / 100, charging: isNap(w) };
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  // ---- record dot + timer -------------------------------------------------------------
    86	  const untz = L.findLine({ match: 'untz', section: 'intro' }) || L.findLine({ match: 'untz' });
    87	  const recStart = untz ? B.snap(untz.start, 'downbeat') : intro.start + B.barDur;
    88	  function rec(t) {
    89	    const tt = loopTime(t);
    90	    if (tt < recStart) return { on: false, secs: 0 };
    91	    return { on: true, secs: tt - recStart };
    92	  }
    93	
    94	  return { clauses, loopTime, loopShift: shiftFrames / fps, windowFor, clauseAt, clock, battery, rec, recStart, intro, outro };
    95	}
     1	// Time of day -> light. Background colour temperature and brightness follow the story
     2	// clock: deep blue night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm, blue again 8pm.
     3	// grade(min) returns a palette of the scene's base colours lit for that minute (cached:
     4	// it depends only on the minute, so it is the same on every worker and frame order).
     5	
     6	import { clamp, lerp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
     7	import { rgbOf, rgb, mix } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
     8	
     9	// [minute, multiplier rgb, lift rgb]
    10	const KEYS = [
    11	  [0,    [0.11, 0.14, 0.30], [4, 6, 16]],
    12	  [270,  [0.11, 0.14, 0.30], [4, 6, 16]],
    13	  [300,  [0.34, 0.39, 0.52], [10, 12, 20]],   // 5am grey-blue
    14	  [345,  [0.55, 0.58, 0.68], [8, 9, 14]],
    15	  [420,  [1.02, 0.88, 0.72], [10, 6, 0]],     // 7am warm
    16	  [540,  [1.03, 0.99, 0.93], [6, 5, 2]],
    17	  [720,  [1.06, 1.06, 1.04], [8, 8, 8]],      // noon white
    18	  [900,  [1.04, 0.98, 0.88], [6, 4, 0]],
    19	  [1080, [1.04, 0.78, 0.46], [16, 6, 0]],     // 6pm gold
    20	  [1140, [0.96, 0.80, 0.64], [8, 4, 0]],      // 7pm lamplight (bath)
    21	  [1170, [0.70, 0.60, 0.56], [4, 3, 4]],      // 7:30 reading, dim lamp
    22	  [1185, [0.22, 0.26, 0.48], [4, 6, 14]],     // 7:45 lights out
    23	  [1200, [0.24, 0.28, 0.52], [4, 6, 16]],     // 8pm blue
    24	  [1320, [0.12, 0.15, 0.31], [4, 6, 16]],
    25	  [1440, [0.11, 0.14, 0.30], [4, 6, 16]],
    26	];
    27	
    28	export function light(min) {
    29	  const m = ((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
    30	  let i = 0;
    31	  while (i < KEYS.length - 2 && KEYS[i + 1][0] <= m) i++;
    32	  const [a, ma, la] = KEYS[i], [b, mb, lb] = KEYS[i + 1];
    33	  const k = clamp((m - a) / (b - a));
    34	  const mul = [0, 1, 2].map((j) => lerp(ma[j], mb[j], k));
    35	  const lift = [0, 1, 2].map((j) => lerp(la[j], lb[j], k));
    36	  const lum = 0.3 * mul[0] + 0.55 * mul[1] + 0.15 * mul[2];
    37	  return { min: m, mul, lift, lum, night: clamp((0.45 - lum) / 0.3), warm: clamp((mul[0] - mul[2]) * 2) };
    38	}
    39	
    40	/** Base (true) colours of the world, before lighting. */
    41	export const BASE = {
    42	  wall: '#d9e4dd', wall2: '#c4d3ca', trim: '#f6f3ee', floor: '#b88d63', rug: '#cfd8d2',
    43	  wood: '#d8a878', woodDark: '#a8774f', crib: '#f1ece4', cribShade: '#cfc5b8', sheet: '#dfe8ef', sheetShade: '#b9c7d3',
    44	  // baby Clawd: terracotta clay, a darker clay for the legs, dark ink outline and eyes
    45	  skin: '#d77757', skinShade: '#b35a3d', skinLight: '#f5b394', hair: '#d77757', ink: '#241715', line: '#2b1c19', mouth: '#4a1f2a', white: '#fff8f0',
    46	  blush: '#f0848a', tear: '#8fd0f6', paci: '#76c6b8', paciRing: '#fbf3e4',
    47	  onesie: '#f7f4ee', onesieShade: '#d9d3c8', sack: '#8fa6c6', sackShade: '#6f86a8', towel: '#ffffff', stain: '#e6d3a4',
    48	  window: '#cfe6ff', sky: '#9ccbf2', curtain: '#e8b9a6', fan: '#f4f1ea', fanShade: '#c9c2b6', metal: '#9a9ea4',
    49	  tray: '#f3f0ea', chair: '#efe4d4', chairShade: '#cdbfa9', tile: '#cfe3e6', tileLine: '#a9c4c8', water: '#bfe6f2',
    50	  bottle: '#f4f8fb', milk: '#fbf8ef', nipple: '#e6c28a', book: '#f2a541', book2: '#4fa3c7', food: '#f0b84a', food2: '#9fcf6a',
    51	  parent: '#3c3f4c', parentSkin: '#c8704f', parentShade: '#a5523a', door: '#efe6d8', doorDark: '#2a2622', alarm: '#2b2b30', lamp: '#ffd9a0',
    52	  plush: '#c9a27a', seat: '#5d6b7a', seatShade: '#465260',
    53	};
    54	
    55	const cache = new Map();
    56	/** Lit palette for a minute. soft < 1 pulls the light toward neutral (used for the baby,
    57	 *  so faces stay readable while the room carries the colour temperature). */
    58	export function grade(min, base = BASE, soft = 1) {
    59	  const key = Math.floor(((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
    60	  const ck = soft === 1 && base === BASE ? key : `${key}:${soft}:${base === BASE ? 'b' : 'x'}`;
    61	  let G = cache.get(ck);
    62	  if (G) return G;
    63	  const L0 = light(key);
    64	  const L = soft === 1 ? L0 : { ...L0, mul: L0.mul.map((m) => 1 + (m - 1) * soft), lift: L0.lift.map((l) => l * soft) };
    65	  G = { light: L };
    66	  for (const [k, c] of Object.entries(base)) {
    67	    const [r, g, b] = rgbOf(c);
    68	    G[k] = rgb(Math.min(255, r * L.mul[0] + L.lift[0]), Math.min(255, g * L.mul[1] + L.lift[1]), Math.min(255, b * L.mul[2] + L.lift[2]));
    69	  }
    70	  // the face's ink stays legible even at night
    71	  G.ink = mix(G.ink, base.ink, 0.6);
    72	  G.light = L0;
    73	  cache.set(ck, G);
    74	  return G;
    75	}
    76	
    77	/** Colours under the phone's flash at night: the face blown out, nothing else lit. */
    78	export const FLASH = (() => {
    79	  const F = {};
    80	  for (const [k, c] of Object.entries(BASE)) F[k] = mix(c, '#ffffff', 0.18);
    81	  F.skin = '#ec9272'; F.skinShade = '#c66a4c'; F.skinLight = '#ffc7aa'; F.ink = '#1b1210'; F.line = '#2a1a16'; F.mouth = '#4a1a22';
    82	  F.blush = '#ff8e94'; F.white = '#ffffff';
    83	  F.sack = '#b9cbe6'; F.sackShade = '#8ea4c6'; F.onesie = '#ffffff'; F.onesieShade = '#dfe3ea';
    84	  return F;
    85	})();

    88	export const SCREEN = (() => {

    92	  F.blush = '#d893b4';

    96	/** The 16:9 backdrop's sun / moon position for a minute (0..1 across the arc, and which). */
    97	export function skyBody(min) {
    98	  const m = ((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
    99	  if (m >= 360 && m < 1140) return { body: 'sun', p: (m - 360) / 780 };
   100	  const n = m >= 1140 ? m - 1140 : m + 300;
   101	  return { body: 'moon', p: n / 660 };
   102	}
     1	// The phone camera: selfie handshake + a slight kick shake + per-clip tilt (owned by the
     2	// compositor, so a clip can ask to be held absolutely still), and the lens post: grain
     3	// (stronger at night, seeded on a fixed 24 Hz tick so 30p and 60p match) and vignette.
     4	
     5	import { hash, noise1, clamp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
     6	import { CW, CH } from './props.js';
     7	
     8	export function cameraTransform(ctx, C, cam = {}) {
     9	  const { t, B } = C;
    10	  let dx = cam.dx || 0, dy = cam.dy || 0, rot = cam.tilt || 0;
    11	  const z = cam.zoom || 1;
    12	  if (!cam.still) {
    13	    const hs = cam.shake ?? 1;
    14	    dx += noise1(t * 0.9, 11) * 9 * hs;
    15	    dy += noise1(t * 0.75, 12) * 11 * hs;
    16	    rot += noise1(t * 0.5, 13) * 0.009 * hs;
    17	    const ks = cam.kick ?? 1;
    18	    const e = B.kickEnv(t, 0.07) * ks;
    19	    if (e > 0.01) {
    20	      const a = hash(B.kickIndex(t), 14) * Math.PI * 2;
    21	      dx += Math.cos(a) * 9 * e;
    22	      dy += Math.sin(a) * 9 * e + 6 * e;
    23	    }
    24	  }
    25	  ctx.translate(CW / 2 + dx, CH / 2 + dy);
    26	  ctx.rotate(rot);
    27	  ctx.scale(z, z);
    28	  ctx.translate(-CW / 2, -CH / 2);
    29	}
    30	
    31	const tiles = new Map();
    32	function noiseTile(size = 160) {
    33	  let c = tiles.get(size);
    34	  if (c) return c;
    35	  c = document.createElement('canvas');
    36	  c.width = c.height = size;
    37	  const g = c.getContext('2d');
    38	  const img = g.createImageData(size, size);
    39	  for (let i = 0; i < size * size; i++) {
    40	    const v = Math.floor(hash(i, 77) * 255);
    41	    img.data[i * 4] = img.data[i * 4 + 1] = img.data[i * 4 + 2] = v;
    42	    img.data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
    43	  }
    44	  g.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
    45	  tiles.set(size, c);
    46	  return c;
    47	}
    48	
    49	export function lensPost(ctx, C, cam = {}) {
    50	  const night = C.G.light.night;
    51	  ctx.save();
    52	  // vignette (phone lenses vignette a little; more in the dark)
    53	  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(CW / 2, CH * 0.52, CH * 0.28, CW / 2, CH * 0.52, CH * 0.75);
    54	  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    55	  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${0.18 + 0.35 * night})`);
    56	  ctx.fillStyle = g;
    57	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
    58	  // grain
    59	  const amt = (cam.grain ?? 1) * (0.022 + 0.045 * night);
    60	  if (amt > 0) {
    61	    const tick = Math.floor(C.t * 24);
    62	    const pat = ctx.createPattern(noiseTile(), 'repeat');
    63	    pat.setTransform(new DOMMatrix().translate(Math.floor(hash(tick, 1) * 160) * 2, Math.floor(hash(tick, 2) * 160) * 2).scale(2));
    64	    ctx.fillStyle = pat;
    65	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'overlay';
    66	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(amt * 2.2);
    67	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
    68	  }
    69	  ctx.restore();
    70	}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Render frames in headless Chrome and encode with ffmpeg.
//
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video [options]
//
//   --layout 9x16|16x9      song layout (default: the song's default)
//   --w 540 --h 960         output size (default: half the layout's design size)
//   --fps 30                frame rate
//   --from 0 --to 10        time range in seconds (default: whole song)
//   --out file.mp4          encode an MP4 (libx264 CRF 16 + AAC from the song's audio)
//   --audio file.wav        audio override (default: picked from data/audio.json "source")
//   --data dir/             timing data dir, relative to the song dir (default data/)
//   --workers 8             parallel Chrome instances (frames are independent)
//   --crf 16 --preset medium
//   --sheet file.png        contact sheet: one cell per timeline window (+ song extras)
//   --sheet-mode clips|even --count 24 --cols 10 --cell-w 216
//   --stills 1.5,23,40      single PNG frames  (--stills-dir dir)
//   --check                 determinism check (ordered vs shuffled across workers) and,
//                           if the song loops (outro = intro), frame-exactness of the loop;
//                           a song that declares an outro loop but has none fails the check
//
// At start-up it prints where line sections came from and which section-derived plates
// (song.sectionPlates, e.g. the bridge long take) exist, with MISSING for absent ones.
//
// Chrome: /Applications/Google Chrome.app (override with CHROME=...). Nothing is downloaded.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { spawn, execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const opt = (k, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + k); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : d; };
const flag = (k) => argv.includes('--' + k);
const CHROME = process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome';

// the song dir as a repo-relative URL path (the page is served from the repo root); absolute paths work too
const songDir = path.relative(REPO, path.resolve(REPO, opt('song', 'songs/2am-crying/video'))).split(path.sep).join('/');
const layout = opt('layout', null);
const fps = Number(opt('fps', 30));
const workersN = Number(opt('workers', Math.max(2, Math.min(10, Math.floor(os.cpus().length * 0.6)))));
const outMp4 = opt('out', null);
const sheetOut = opt('sheet', null);
const stills = opt('stills', null);
const check = flag('check');
const dataDir = opt('data', null);
let failures = 0;

const abs = (p) => (path.isAbsolute(p) ? p : path.join(REPO, p));
const log = (...a) => console.log('[render]', ...a);

function ffprobeDuration(file) {
  try { return Number(execFileSync('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-show_entries', 'format=duration', '-of', 'csv=p=0', file]).toString().trim()); }
  catch { return NaN; }
}

function pickAudio(audioJson) {
  const given = opt('audio', null);
  const dur = audioJson.duration;
  const bar = (60 / audioJson.bpm) * (audioJson.beats_per_bar || 4);
  let file, why;
  if (given) { file = abs(given); why = '--audio'; }
  else if (String(audioJson.source || '').startsWith('synth')) {
    const dir = path.join(abs(songDir), '..', 'synth', 'out');
    const wavs = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.wav')).map((f) => path.join(dir, f)) : [];
    const scored = wavs.map((f) => ({ f, d: ffprobeDuration(f) })).filter((x) => isFinite(x.d)).sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.d - dur) - Math.abs(b.d - dur));
    if (scored.length) { file = scored[0].f; why = 'synth (closest duration to audio.json)'; }
  }
  if (!file) { file = path.join(abs(songDir), 'audio', 'click.wav'); why = 'click track (audio.json source: ' + (audioJson.source || '?') + ')'; }
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`audio ${file} not found (generate the click track: node ${songDir}/tools/make_click.mjs)`);
  const d = ffprobeDuration(file);
  if (Math.abs(d - dur) > bar && !flag('force')) {
    throw new Error(`audio ${path.relative(REPO, file)} is ${d.toFixed(2)}s but data/audio.json says ${dur.toFixed(2)}s (more than a bar apart): the timing and the audio do not match. Regenerate timing or pass --force.`);
  }
  return { file, why, d };
}

async function launchWorker(port, w, h, i) {
  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
    executablePath: CHROME,
    headless: true,
    args: ['--hide-scrollbars', '--mute-audio', '--force-device-scale-factor=1', '--disable-background-timer-throttling',
      '--disable-renderer-backgrounding', '--disable-backgrounding-occluded-windows', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run'],
    defaultViewport: { width: Math.min(w, 1920), height: Math.min(h, 1920), deviceScaleFactor: 1 },
    protocolTimeout: 600000,
  });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  const errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(String(e)));
  page.on('console', (m) => { if ((m.type() === 'error' && !/status of 404/.test(m.text())) || m.type() === 'warn' || m.type() === 'warning') errors.push(m.text()); });
  page.on('response', (r) => { if (r.status() >= 400 && !/favicon/.test(r.url())) errors.push(`HTTP ${r.status()} ${r.url()}`); });
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ render: '1', w: String(w), h: String(h), fps: String(fps) });
  if (layout) q.set('layout', layout);
  if (dataDir) q.set('data', dataDir.replace(/\/?$/, '/'));
  try {
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${songDir}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 120000 });
    await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 120000 });
  } catch (e) {
    await closeWorker({ browser });
    throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page not ready: ${e.message}${errors.length ? '\n  ' + errors.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  }
  const info = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__render.error ? { error: window.__render.error } : {
    duration: window.__render.duration, windows: window.__render.windows, w: window.__render.w, h: window.__render.h, layout: window.__render.layout,
    sections: window.__render.sections,
  }));
  if (info.error) throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page failed: ${info.error}\n${errors.join('\n')}`);
  if (i === 0 && errors.length) log('page messages:\n  ' + errors.join('\n  '));
  return { browser, page, info, errors };
}

/** Close a worker's browser, but never hang on it: kill whatever has not exited after 5 s. */
async function closeWorker(x) {
  const proc = x.browser.process();
  await Promise.race([x.browser.close().catch(() => {}), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000))]);
  if (proc && proc.exitCode == null) proc.kill('SIGKILL');
}

async function main() {
  const audioJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(abs(songDir), dataDir || 'data', 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));
  const server = createServer(REPO);
  const port = await listen(server, 0);
  // output size from the layout's design size unless given
  let w = Number(opt('w', 0)), h = Number(opt('h', 0));
  if (!w || !h) {
    const portrait = (layout || '9x16') === '9x16';
    w = w || (portrait ? 540 : 960); h = h || (portrait ? 960 : 540);
  }
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const nW = outMp4 ? workersN : 1;
  // launch all workers; if any fails to start, close the ones that did (no orphaned Chromes)
  const launched = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));
  const bad = launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'rejected');
  if (bad.length) {
    await Promise.all(launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled').map((x) => closeWorker(x.value)));
    server.closeAllConnections?.(); server.close();
    throw bad[0].reason;
  }
  const workers = launched.map((x) => x.value);
  const info = workers[0].info;
  log(`${workers.length} worker(s) ready in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s · ${info.w}x${info.h} @${fps} · layout ${info.layout} · song ${info.duration.toFixed(2)}s · ${info.windows.length} windows`);
  const P = workers[0].page;
  reportSections(info);

  try {
    if (stills) {
      const dir = abs(opt('stills-dir', path.join(songDir, 'renders', 'stills')));
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      for (const s of stills.split(',').map(Number)) {
        const url = await P.evaluate((t) => window.__render.png(t), s);
        const f = path.join(dir, `still-${info.layout}-${s.toFixed(3)}.png`);
        fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
        log('still', path.relative(REPO, f));
      }
    }

    if (sheetOut) {
      const mode = opt('sheet-mode', 'clips');
      let cells;
      if (mode === 'even') {
        const n = Number(opt('count', 24));
        cells = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ t: (info.duration * (i + 0.5)) / n, label: `#${i + 1}` }));
      } else cells = await P.evaluate(() => window.__render.heroCells());
      const portrait = info.h > info.w;
      const cols = Number(opt('cols', portrait ? 10 : 5));
      const cellW = Number(opt('cell-w', portrait ? 216 : 384));
      const title = opt('title', `${songDir} · ${info.layout} · ${cells.length} cells · ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16)}`);
      const url = await P.evaluate((c, o) => window.__render.sheet(c, o), cells, { cols, cellW, title });
      const f = abs(sheetOut);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      log(`sheet (${cells.length} cells) -> ${path.relative(REPO, f)}`);
    }

    if (check) await runChecks(workers, info);

    if (outMp4) await encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson);
  } finally {
    await Promise.all(workers.map(closeWorker));
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    server.close();
  }
}

/** Where line sections came from, and which section-derived plates exist (loud when missing). */
function reportSections(info) {
  const S = info.sections || {};
  const fmt = (t) => `${Math.floor(t / 60)}:${(t % 60).toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`;
  log(`line sections from ${S.source || '?'}: ${(S.list || []).join(', ')}`);
  for (const sp of S.expect || []) {
    const ws = info.windows.filter((x) => x.fromSection === sp.label);
    if (ws.length) log(`section plate ok: ${sp.label} -> ${ws.map((x) => `${x.plate}${x.params.mode ? ':' + x.params.mode : ''} ${fmt(x.start)}-${fmt(x.end)} (${(x.end - x.start).toFixed(1)}s)`).join(', ')}`);
    else log(`section plate MISSING: ${sp.label} (no lyric line in section "${sp.section}")`);
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Smoke test for the preview page: loads index.html in headless Chrome with each given
// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --q "t=23" --q "layout=16x9&t=40" [--shots dir]
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const song = argv.includes('--song') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--song') + 1] : 'songs/2am-crying/video';
const qs = argv.flatMap((a, i) => (a === '--q' ? [argv[i + 1]] : []));
const shots = argv.includes('--shots') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--shots') + 1] : null;
const srv = createServer(REPO);
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 1000 } });
let bad = 0;
for (const q of qs.length ? qs : ['t=0']) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
  p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/favicon|404/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${song}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__preview || (window.__render && window.__render.error)', { timeout: 30000 });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
  const info = await p.evaluate(() => ({ err: document.getElementById('pv-err')?.textContent || '', bar: [...document.querySelectorAll('.pv-bar span')].map((s) => s.textContent).join(' | ') }));
  if (info.err || errs.length) bad++;
  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();
}
await b.close();
srv.close();
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
async function runChecks(workers, info) {
  const P = workers[0].page;
  const ts = [0.5, info.duration * 0.21, info.duration * 0.37, info.duration * 0.55, info.duration * 0.71, info.duration * 0.88].map((t) => Math.round(t * fps) / fps);
  const ordered = [];
  for (const t of ts) ordered.push(await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t));
  const shuffled = [...ts].map((t, i) => ({ t, i })).sort((a, b) => ((a.i * 7919) % 13) - ((b.i * 7919) % 13));
  // on a fresh worker (or the same one, out of order) with unrelated frames in between
  const Q = workers[workers.length > 1 ? 1 : 0].page;
  const hashes = {};
  for (const { t, i } of shuffled) { await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), info.duration - t); hashes[i] = await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t); }
  const bad = ts.filter((_, i) => hashes[i] !== ordered[i]);
  log(bad.length ? `DETERMINISM FAIL at ${bad.join(', ')}` : `determinism ok (${ts.length} frames, ordered vs shuffled)`);
  failures += bad.length;
  const intro = info.windows[0], outro = info.windows.find((x) => x.plate === intro.plate && x.params.loop);
  const wantsLoop = (info.sections?.expect || []).some((sp) => /loop/.test(sp.label));
  if (!outro && wantsLoop) { failures++; log('LOOP FAIL: the song declares an outro loop but the timeline has no loop window'); }
  if (outro) {
    const outroStartFrame = Math.ceil(outro.start * fps - 1e-6);
    let fails = 0;
    let shown = null;
    for (const j of [3, 17, 40, 60, 90, 150]) {
      const k = outroStartFrame + j;
      if (k / fps >= info.duration) continue;
      // the intro frame the loop maps onto: the song says (e.g. anchored on a lyric), else outro start -> intro start
      const src = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.loopSource(x), k / fps);
      const jj = src == null ? Math.round(k - outro.start * fps) : Math.round(src * fps);
      if (src != null && (Math.abs(src * fps - jj) > 1e-6 || src >= outro.start)) { fails++; log(`LOOP FAIL: outro frame ${k} maps to t=${src}, not an intro frame`); continue; }
      const a = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), jj / fps);
      const b = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), k / fps);
      if (a !== b) { fails++; log(`LOOP MISMATCH: outro frame ${k} vs intro frame ${jj}`); }
      shown ??= { k, jj };
    }
    if (shown) log(`loop mapping: outro frame ${shown.k} (${(shown.k / fps).toFixed(3)}s) -> intro frame ${shown.jj} (${(shown.jj / fps).toFixed(3)}s), shift ${((shown.k - shown.jj) / fps).toFixed(3)}s`);
    failures += fails;
    log(fails ? `loop check: ${fails} mismatches` : `loop ok (outro frames are intro frames; outro window ${outro.start.toFixed(2)}s, intro ${intro.start.toFixed(2)}s)`);
  }
}

  const to = Math.min(Number(opt('to', info.duration)), info.duration);
  const f0 = Math.round(from * fps), f1 = Math.round(to * fps);
  const N = f1 - f0;

// Math, easing, envelopes and seeded hashing. Everything here is pure: a frame is a
// function of song time, so nothing may carry state between calls.

export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
export const clamp = (x, a = 0, b = 1) => (x < a ? a : x > b ? b : x);
export const lerp = (a, b, k) => a + (b - a) * k;
export const invLerp = (a, b, x) => (b === a ? 0 : (x - a) / (b - a));
/** Progress of t through [a, b], clamped to 0..1. */
export const seg = (t, a, b) => clamp(invLerp(a, b, t));
export const smooth = (k) => { k = clamp(k); return k * k * (3 - 2 * k); };
/** 1 inside [a, b], with linear fades of fi / fo seconds at the edges. */
export const trap = (t, a, b, fi = 0.1, fo = 0.1) =>
  Math.min(fi > 0 ? clamp((t - a) / fi) : (t >= a ? 1 : 0), fo > 0 ? clamp((b - t) / fo) : (t < b ? 1 : 0));

export const ease = {
  linear: (k) => k,
  inQuad: (k) => k * k,
  outQuad: (k) => 1 - (1 - k) * (1 - k),
  inOutQuad: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2),
  inCubic: (k) => k * k * k,
  outCubic: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3),
  inOutCubic: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 4 * k * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 3) / 2),
  outQuart: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 4),
  inQuart: (k) => k * k * k * k,
  inExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : Math.pow(2, 10 * k - 10)),
  outExpo: (k) => (k >= 1 ? 1 : 1 - Math.pow(2, -10 * k)),
  inOutExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : k < 0.5 ? Math.pow(2, 20 * k - 10) / 2 : (2 - Math.pow(2, -20 * k + 10)) / 2),
  outBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => 1 + (s + 1) * Math.pow(k - 1, 3) + s * Math.pow(k - 1, 2),
  inBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => (s + 1) * k * k * k - s * k * k,
  outElastic: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : Math.pow(2, -10 * k) * Math.sin((k * 10 - 0.75) * (TAU / 3)) + 1),
  outBounce: (k) => {
    const n = 7.5625, d = 2.75;
    if (k < 1 / d) return n * k * k;
    if (k < 2 / d) return n * (k -= 1.5 / d) * k + 0.75;
    if (k < 2.5 / d) return n * (k -= 2.25 / d) * k + 0.9375;
    return n * (k -= 2.625 / d) * k + 0.984375;
  },
};
/** Eased progress of t through [a, b]. */
export const eseg = (t, a, b, fn = ease.outCubic) => fn(seg(t, a, b));

/**
 * Envelope that peaks (=1) at `at`: linear attack over `attack` seconds before it,
 * exponential decay with time constant `decay` after it. 0 far away.
 */

p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/nap.js'
s=open(p).read()

head_old=s[:start]

head_new = head_old.replace("""// Naps. mode 'tip' (11am): hard cut to daylight, eyes closed, the phone slowly tipping
// over until it looks up at the ceiling fan. mode 'carseat' (3pm): head at an angle in the
// car seat. mode 'long' (the bridge, one long take): bottle, eyes getting heavy, the phone

// over until it looks up at the ceiling fan. mode 'carseat' (3pm): head at an angle in the
// car seat. mode 'long' (the bridge, one long take, no kick, almost whispered): held in the
// parent's arms (an adult Clawd: its body behind, one nub behind the shoulder, one across

// 10:55 heavy lids, settling lower; 10:58 "I love you": the eyes leave the phone for the

import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';

assert head_new != head_old

# replace fanAngle helper? keep (unused by long now) -> remove fanAngle since only long used it
import re
fa_start = head_new.index('/** Fan angle with speed ramping')
fa_end = head_new.index('function lyingBaby')
head_new = head_new[:fa_start] + head_new[fa_end:]

long_code = r'''
// ---- the bridge long take ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Every stage time comes from the bridge clauses (their time stamps first, then their words),
// with fallbacks as fractions of the window, forced into order with minimum gaps so another
// take can only move the stages, never invert them. Words are searched over all the bridge
// clauses (not C.words, which only holds the clause on screen).
const TIMES = new WeakMap();
function bridgeTimes(C) {
  const key = C.win;
  if (TIMES.has(key)) return TIMES.get(key);
  const S = C.start, E = C.end, D = C.dur;
  const cl = C.story.clauses.filter((c) => c.section === 'bridge');
  const all = cl.flatMap((c) => c.words);
  const clause = (...res) => { for (const re of res) { const c = cl.find((x) => re.test(x.n)); if (c) return c.start; } return null; };
  const word = (m, after = -Infinity, backing = false) => {
    const w = all.find((x) => !!x.backing === backing && x.start >= after && norm(x.w).includes(m));
    return w ? w.start : null;
  };
  const T = {};
  T.hold = clamp(clause(/^its 10:50\b/, /holding/) ?? S, S, E);
  T.drink = clamp(clause(/^its 10:52\b/, /drinking/) ?? S + 0.15 * D, S, E);
  T.heavy = clamp(clause(/^its 10:55\b/, /heavy/) ?? S + 0.33 * D, S, E);
  T.love = clamp(clause(/^its 10:58\b/, /love/) ?? S + 0.55 * D, S, E);
  T.nap = clamp(clause(/^its 11am\b/, /napping/) ?? S + 0.8 * D, S, E);
  // order, with room for each stage; the nap needs the last ~2.4 s for the tip and the hold
  T.drink = Math.max(T.drink, T.hold);
  T.heavy = Math.max(T.heavy, T.drink + 1.0);
  T.love = Math.max(T.love, T.heavy + 1.5);
  T.nap = Math.min(Math.max(T.nap, T.love + 1.5), E - 2.4);
  T.love = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.love, T.nap - 1.0));
  T.heavy = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.heavy, T.love - 0.8));
  T.drink = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.drink, T.heavy - 0.5));
  const within = (x, a, b, f) => (x == null || x < a || x > b ? f : x);
  T.warmWord = within(word('warm', T.drink), T.drink, T.love, T.drink + 0.6 * (T.heavy - T.drink));
  T.heavyWord = within(word('heavy', T.heavy), T.heavy, T.love, T.heavy + 0.6 * (T.love - T.heavy));
  T.loveWord = within(word('love', T.love), T.love, T.nap, T.love + 0.5 * (T.nap - T.love));
  // the parenthetical after "I love you" (a backing line), else halfway to the nap
  T.paren = within(word('', T.loveWord + 0.05, true), T.loveWord, T.nap, lerp(T.loveWord, T.nap, 0.4));
  // the tip: a beat after the eyes close, slow, done a second before the cut (the quiet hold)
  T.tipB = Math.min(T.nap + 2.8, E - 1.0);
  T.tipA = Math.max(T.nap + 0.3, Math.min(T.nap + 1.0, T.tipB - 1.2));
  TIMES.set(key, T);
  return T;
}

/** The long take's curves at C.t (shared by camera() and draw()). */
function bridgeState(C) {
  const T = bridgeTimes(C);
  const t = C.t, S = C.start;
  const q = ease.inOutQuad;
  const s = { T };
  // the parent's chest: one slow breath every ~4.6 s, never on the beat
  s.breath = Math.sin(TAU * (t - S) / 4.6);
  // the bottle comes up (already there when the take opens on the drinking line)
  const b0 = T.drink <= S + 0.3 ? S - 2 : T.drink;
  s.bottleIn = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, b0, b0 + 1.4));
  s.level = lerp(0.8, 0.22, seg(t, T.drink + 0.8, T.nap));
  s.blush = 0.18 + 0.37 * q(seg(t, T.warmWord - 0.2, T.warmWord + 1.8));
  s.settle = q(seg(t, T.heavy + 0.4, T.heavyWord + 1.4));
  s.lookUp = q(seg(t, T.love + 0.35, T.love + 1.5)) * (1 - q(seg(t, T.nap, T.nap + 1.0)));
  s.lean = q(seg(t, T.love + 0.9, T.love + 2.9));
  s.close = q(seg(t, T.nap, T.nap + 1.1));
  s.slip = q(seg(t, T.nap + 0.4, T.nap + 1.9));
  s.open = clamp((1 - 0.12 * seg(t, T.drink + 0.6, T.drink + 2.0) - 0.43 * q(seg(t, T.heavy + 0.6, T.heavyWord + 0.9))
    + 0.2 * q(seg(t, T.love + 0.35, T.love + 1.5)) - 0.3 * q(seg(t, T.paren, T.nap + 0.2))) * (1 - s.close));
  s.tip = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tipA, T.tipB));
  s.glow = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, T.tipA + 0.3, C.end));
  return s;
}

const HUG = { u: 128, x: 560, top0: -470, top1: -70 };   // the parent's block: unit, centre, top edge before / after the lean

/** The room above the parent: the wall band and the ceiling frame (the tip ends there). */
function heldRoom(ctx, C, s) {
  const { G } = C;
  const wallC = mix(G.wall, '#f3c99a', 0.28);
  ctx.fillStyle = wallC;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(0, -UP);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
  ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 900, R: 520, angle: 0.5 + 0.35 * (C.t - C.start), blur: 0 });
  ctx.restore();
  // the trim where the wall meets the ceiling
  ctx.fillStyle = mix(G.trim, '#f3c99a', 0.2);
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -BAND - 26, CW + 1800, 26);
}

function heldTake(ctx, C) {
  const { G } = C;
  const s = bridgeState(C);
  const br = s.breath;
  const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient');
  heldRoom(ctx, C, s);
  // ---- the parent's body behind the baby, a little out of focus and in the baby's shadow ----
  const parentClay = mix(G.parentSkin, '#4a2016', 0.24), parentDk = mix(G.parentShade, '#3a160f', 0.3);
  const top = lerp(HUG.top0, HUG.top1, s.lean) - 30 * s.settle + 8 * br;
  const ey = top + 2 * HUG.u;
  const m = ctx.getTransform();
  const px = Math.hypot(m.a, m.b);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(HUG.x, ey);
  ctx.rotate(-0.03 * s.lean);
  ctx.scale(1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br, 1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br);
  ctx.filter = `blur(${(3 * px).toFixed(2)}px)`;
  adultClawd(ctx, {
    x: 0, y: (24 - 2) * HUG.u, u: HUG.u, pose: 'sit', h: 24, clay: parentClay, clayDk: parentDk, ink: G.line,
    eyes: 'open', eyeColor: G.ink, shine: G.white, look: [-0.15, 0.75], arms: null,
  });
  ctx.filter = 'none';
  ctx.restore();
  // ---- the nub behind the baby's shoulder (left), the baby, the nub across its legs (right) ----
  const armY = 6 * br;
  hand(ctx, G, { x: 330, y: 1090 + armY + 20 * s.settle, a: Math.PI + 0.2, s: 0.95, color: mix(G.parentSkin, '#4a2016', 0.12) });
  const bx = SELFIE.x - 10 * s.settle, by = SELFIE.y + 62 * s.settle + 5 * br;
  const tilt = -0.05 * s.settle;
  const r = SELFIE.r;
  // the bottle: from below to the mouth, a slow suck, then slipping onto the parent's arm
  const sip = 0.025 * Math.sin((C.t - C.start) * 5.2) * (1 - s.slip);
  const P0 = { x: 520, y: 1000, a: -0.15 }, P1 = { x: 95, y: 268, a: -0.62 + sip }, P2 = { x: 330, y: 560, a: 0.62 };
  const lp = (a, b, k) => ({ x: lerp(a.x, b.x, k), y: lerp(a.y, b.y, k), a: lerp(a.a, b.a, k) });
  const pose = lp(lp(P0, P1, s.bottleIn), P2, s.slip);
  const bs = 1.05;
  // where the free nub grips the bottle (its body, 150 down the axis), in head units
  const grip = [(pose.x - Math.sin(pose.a) * 150 * bs) / r, (pose.y + Math.cos(pose.a) * 150 * bs) / r];
  const rest = [1.02, 1.3], lap = [0.98, 1.42];
  const k = s.bottleIn * (1 - s.slip);
  const at = [lerp(lerp(rest[0], grip[0], s.bottleIn), lap[0], s.slip), lerp(lerp(rest[1], grip[1], s.bottleIn), lap[1], s.slip)];
  const covered = k > 0.6;
  drawBaby(ctx, {
    x: bx, y: by, r, tilt, C: L, outfit: 'onesie',
    eyes: 'open', open: s.open, look: [0.15 * s.lookUp, -1.6 * s.lookUp], blush: s.blush,
    mouth: covered ? 'none' : s.slip > 0.3 ? 'o' : 'smile', mouthOpen: 0.22,
    free: { mode: 'hold', at },
  });
  hand(ctx, G, { x: 300, y: 1712 + armY, a: 0.1, s: 1.0 });
  if (s.bottleIn > 0) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(bx, by); ctx.rotate(tilt);
    bottle(ctx, G, { x: pose.x, y: pose.y, a: pose.a, s: bs, level: s.level });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // ---- warm, soft light: a warm cast over everything and a key from the window, up left ----
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'soft-light';
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffa45c', 0.45);
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(80, 380, 60, 80, 380, 1500);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffd9a6', 0.3));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffd9a6', 0));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
  ctx.restore();
}
'''

head_new = head_new.rstrip('\n') + '\n' + long_code + '\n'

# body: replace camera long-take part, caption, draw long-take part, add overlay
b_old_cam = body[body.index("    // long take: a degree per beat"):body.index("  caption(C) {")]

    // heavy; at 11am the tip-over up past the parent to the ceiling, then breathing only
    const s = bridgeState(C);
    const k = s.tip;
    return {
      dy: UP * 0.97 * k + 5 * s.breath * k,
      tilt: (0.012 * Math.sin((C.t - C.start) * 0.45) + 0.035 * s.settle) * (1 - k) + 0.08 * k + 0.22 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k),
      zoom: 1 - 0.07 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: lerp(0.3, 0.05, k), kick: 0,
    };

  },
  overlay(ctx, C) {
    if (C.params.mode !== 'long') return;
    // fading toward the warm light (screen space, whatever the camera is doing)
    const a = 0.55 * bridgeState(C).glow;
    if (a <= 0.002) return;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(CW * 0.5, CH * 0.42, 80, CW * 0.5, CH * 0.42, CH * 0.8);
    g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#fff4dc', a));
    g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffc98a', a * 0.85));
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
  },
""")

    19	
    20	/** The tip-over strip: lying baby (y >= 0), a wall band, then the ceiling frame above. */
    21	function tipStack(ctx, C, drawLying, fan) {
    22	  const { G } = C;
    23	  ctx.save();
    24	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, 0, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
    25	  drawLying();
    26	  ctx.restore();
    27	  // band: the crib's far rail against the wall
    28	  ctx.fillStyle = G.wall;
    29	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -BAND, CW + 1800, BAND);
    30	  ctx.fillStyle = G.crib;
    31	  ctx.fillRect(-900, -110, CW + 1800, 80);
    32	  for (let x = -600; x < CW + 600; x += 110) ctx.fillRect(x, -60, 40, 60);
    33	  ctx.save();
    34	  ctx.translate(0, -UP);
    35	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
    36	  ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 900, R: 520, ...fan });
    37	  ctx.restore();
    38	}
    39	
    40	function lyingBaby(ctx, C, o = {}) {
    41	  // the baby on its back on the crib sheet, seen from the phone held above its face
    42	  const { G } = C;
    43	  fillAll(ctx, G.sheet);
    44	  // sheet folds
    45	  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(G.sheetShade, 0.55);
    46	  ctx.lineWidth = 10;
    47	  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-200 + i * 260, 300); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-60 + i * 260, 900, -220 + i * 260, 1900); ctx.stroke(); }
    48	  drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line', ...o });
    49	}
    50	
    51	// ---- the bridge long take ----------------------------------------------------------------
    52	// Every stage time comes from the bridge clauses (their time stamps first, then their words),
    53	// with fallbacks as fractions of the window, forced into order with minimum gaps so another
    54	// take can only move the stages, never invert them. Words are searched over all the bridge
    55	// clauses (not C.words, which only holds the clause on screen).
    56	const TIMES = new WeakMap();
    57	function bridgeTimes(C) {
    58	  const key = C.win;
    59	  if (TIMES.has(key)) return TIMES.get(key);
    60	  const S = C.start, E = C.end, D = C.dur;
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffd9a6', 0.3));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffd9a6', 0));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
  ctx.restore();
}

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.mode === 'carseat') return { shake: 0.6, kick: 0.4, tilt: 0.04 * Math.sin(C.t * 7) * 0.2 };
    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.15, C.end - C.dur * 0.05));
      return { dy: UP * 0.97 * k, tilt: 0.3 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k) + 0.12 * k, zoom: 1 - 0.1 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: 0.4, kick: 0.25 };
    }
    // long take: no kick, a faint handshake, the phone drooping a little as the arm gets
    // heavy; at 11am the tip-over up past the parent to the ceiling, then breathing only
    const s = bridgeState(C);
    const k = s.tip;
    return {
      dy: UP * 0.97 * k + 5 * s.breath * k,
      tilt: (0.012 * Math.sin((C.t - C.start) * 0.45) + 0.035 * s.settle) * (1 - k) + 0.08 * k + 0.22 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k),
      zoom: 1 - 0.07 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: lerp(0.3, 0.05, k), kick: 0,
    };
  },
  caption(C) {
    return C.params.mode === 'long' ? { family: '"Inter", sans-serif' } : {};
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G } = C;
    if (p.mode === 'carseat') {
      // car window behind, daylight, the seat shell around the head
      fillAll(ctx, mix(G.seatShade, '#000000', 0.2));
      windowPane(ctx, G, { x: -80, y: 120, w: 1240, h: 640, curtains: false });
      const tilt = 0.38;
      carSeat(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x, y: SELFIE.y, r: SELFIE.r, tilt });
      const x = SELFIE.x + 60, y = SELFIE.y + 30;
      drawBaby(ctx, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.25, pacifier: 1, tilt });
      carSeatStraps(ctx, G, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r, tilt });
      return;
    }
    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
      tipStack(ctx, C, () => lyingBaby(ctx, C, { pacifier: 1 }), { angle: C.t * 3.2, blur: 0.7 });
      return;
    }
    heldTake(ctx, C);
  },
  overlay(ctx, C) {
    if (C.params.mode !== 'long') return;
    // fading toward the warm light (screen space, whatever the camera is doing)
    const a = 0.55 * bridgeState(C).glow;
    if (a <= 0.002) return;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(CW * 0.5, CH * 0.42, 80, CW * 0.5, CH * 0.42, CH * 0.8);
    g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#fff4dc', a));
    g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffc98a', a * 0.85));
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
  },
};

#!/bin/bash
# usage: stills.sh outdir [data] [layout] -- renders the bridge check times and tiles them

  if (o.lidSoft && lid < 1) {
    // soft heavy eyes: the eye shortens from the top, keeping its round corners, no lid line
    const hh = h * Math.max(lid, 0.22), yy = y0 + h - hh, kr = Math.min(E.rad * s * r, hh / 2);
    ctx.save();
    rr(ctx, x0, yy, w, hh, kr); ctx.fill(); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = C.white || '#fff';
    const hr = w * 0.26 * Math.min(1, 0.45 + 0.55 * lid);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.33 + o.look[0] * 0.02 * r, yy + Math.max(hr * 1.05, hh * 0.27), hr, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    if (lid > 0.45) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.7, y0 + h * 0.68, w * 0.11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/nap.js'
s=open(p).read()
rep("""open: s.open, lidSag: 1, look:""", """open: s.open, lidSoft: true, look:""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print('ok')
EOF

  the scene gives, where the scene draws the prop), `point`, `none`.
- outfits: `sack` (night: a soft bag over the lower body and the legs, zipped up the middle),
  `onesie` (day: a cloth band across the lower body with two snaps), `naked` (bare clay with
  Clawd's darker band, after the bath), `towel` (a cape behind).
- expressions on the same API as before: eyes `open` / `half` / continuous `open` (a heavy lid
  that droops toward the outer corner) / `closed` (a soft arc) / `squeeze` (> <) / `wide` / `focus`
  (a narrow slit); mouth `line` / `o` (the crying O, with a tongue when wide open) / `smile` /
  `grin` / `press` (a flat crimped line, implies a furrowed brow) / `none`; `tears` (thick streams
  and flicking drops, the value is the animation phase), `blush`, `flash`, `stain` (milk on the

// ---- the bridge long take ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Every stage time comes from the bridge clauses (their time stamps first, then their words),
// with fallbacks as fractions of the window, forced into order with minimum gaps so another
// take can only move the stages, never invert them. Words are searched over all the bridge
// clauses (not C.words, which only holds the clause on screen).
const TIMES = new WeakMap();
function bridgeTimes(C) {
  const key = C.win;
  if (TIMES.has(key)) return TIMES.get(key);
  const S = C.start, E = C.end, D = C.dur;
  const cl = C.story.clauses.filter((c) => c.section === 'bridge');
  const all = cl.flatMap((c) => c.words);
  const clause = (...res) => { for (const re of res) { const c = cl.find((x) => re.test(x.n)); if (c) return c.start; } return null; };
  const word = (m, after = -Infinity, backing = false) => {
    const w = all.find((x) => !!x.backing === backing && x.start >= after && norm(x.w).includes(m));
    return w ? w.start : null;
  };
  const T = {};
  T.hold = clamp(clause(/^its 10:50\b/, /holding/) ?? S, S, E);
  T.drink = clamp(clause(/^its 10:52\b/, /drinking/) ?? S + 0.15 * D, S, E);
  T.heavy = clamp(clause(/^its 10:55\b/, /heavy/) ?? S + 0.33 * D, S, E);
  T.love = clamp(clause(/^its 10:58\b/, /love/) ?? S + 0.55 * D, S, E);
  T.nap = clamp(clause(/^its 11am\b/, /napping/) ?? S + 0.8 * D, S, E);
  // order, with room for each stage; the nap needs the last ~2.4 s for the tip and the hold
  T.drink = Math.max(T.drink, T.hold);
  T.heavy = Math.max(T.heavy, T.drink + 1.0);
  T.love = Math.max(T.love, T.heavy + 1.5);
  T.nap = Math.min(Math.max(T.nap, T.love + 1.5), E - 2.4);
  T.love = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.love, T.nap - 1.0));
  T.heavy = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.heavy, T.love - 0.8));
  T.drink = Math.max(S, Math.min(T.drink, T.heavy - 0.5));
  const within = (x, a, b, f) => (x == null || x < a || x > b ? f : x);
  T.warmWord = within(word('warm', T.drink), T.drink, T.love, T.drink + 0.6 * (T.heavy - T.drink));
  T.heavyWord = within(word('heavy', T.heavy), T.heavy, T.love, T.heavy + 0.6 * (T.love - T.heavy));
  T.loveWord = within(word('love', T.love), T.love, T.nap, T.love + 0.5 * (T.nap - T.love));
  // the parenthetical after "I love you" (a backing line), else halfway to the nap
  T.paren = within(word('', T.loveWord + 0.05, true), T.loveWord, T.nap, lerp(T.loveWord, T.nap, 0.4));
  // the tip: a beat after the eyes close, slow, done a second before the cut (the quiet hold)
  T.tipB = Math.min(T.nap + 2.8, E - 1.0);
  T.tipA = Math.max(T.nap + 0.3, Math.min(T.nap + 1.0, T.tipB - 1.2));
  TIMES.set(key, T);
  return T;
}

/** The long take's curves at C.t (shared by camera() and draw()). */
function bridgeState(C) {
  const T = bridgeTimes(C);
  const t = C.t, S = C.start;
  const q = ease.inOutQuad;
  const s = { T };
  // the parent's chest: one slow breath every ~4.6 s, never on the beat
  s.breath = Math.sin(TAU * (t - S) / 4.6);
  // the bottle comes up (already there when the take opens on the drinking line)
  const b0 = T.drink <= S + 0.3 ? S - 2 : T.drink;
  s.bottleIn = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, b0, b0 + 1.4));
  s.level = lerp(0.8, 0.22, seg(t, T.drink + 0.8, T.nap));
  s.blush = 0.18 + 0.54 * q(seg(t, T.warmWord - 0.2, T.warmWord + 1.8));
  s.settle = q(seg(t, T.heavy + 0.4, T.heavyWord + 1.4));
  s.lookUp = q(seg(t, T.love + 0.35, T.love + 1.5)) * (1 - q(seg(t, T.nap, T.nap + 1.0)));
  s.lean = q(seg(t, T.love + 0.9, T.love + 2.9));
  s.close = q(seg(t, T.nap, T.nap + 1.1));
  s.slip = q(seg(t, T.nap + 0.4, T.nap + 1.9));
  // lids: heavy at 10:55, lifted again to look up at the parent, heavier through the parenthetical
  s.open = clamp((1 - 0.5 * q(seg(t, T.heavy + 0.6, T.heavyWord + 0.9))
    + 0.5 * q(seg(t, T.love + 0.2, T.love + 1.3)) - 0.42 * q(seg(t, T.paren, T.nap + 0.2))) * (1 - s.close));
  if (s.open > 0.97) s.open = 1;   // no lid line when they are (nearly) wide open
  s.tip = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.tipA, T.tipB));
  s.glow = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, T.tipA + 0.3, C.end));
  return s;
}

const HUG = { u: 100, x: 610, top0: -380, top1: 50 };   // the parent's block: unit, centre, top edge before / after the lean

/** The room above the parent: the wall band and the ceiling frame (the tip ends there). */
function heldRoom(ctx, C, s) {
  const { G } = C;
  const wallC = mix(G.wall, '#f3c99a', 0.28);
  ctx.fillStyle = wallC;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(0, -UP);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 900); ctx.clip();
  ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 900, R: 520, angle: 0.5 + 0.35 * (C.t - C.start), blur: 0 });
  ctx.restore();
  // the trim where the wall meets the ceiling
  ctx.fillStyle = mix(G.trim, '#f3c99a', 0.2);
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -BAND - 26, CW + 1800, 26);
}

function heldTake(ctx, C) {
  const { G } = C;
  const s = bridgeState(C);
  const br = s.breath;
  const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient');
  heldRoom(ctx, C, s);
  // ---- the parent's body behind the baby, a little out of focus and in the baby's shadow ----
  const parentClay = mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.34), parentDk = mix(G.parentShade, '#2e120c', 0.4);
  const top = lerp(HUG.top0, HUG.top1, s.lean) - 30 * s.settle + 8 * br;
  const ey = top + 2 * HUG.u;
  const m = ctx.getTransform();
  const px = Math.hypot(m.a, m.b);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(HUG.x, ey);
  ctx.rotate(-0.03 * s.lean);
  ctx.scale(1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br, 1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br);
  ctx.filter = `blur(${(2 * px).toFixed(2)}px)`;
  adultClawd(ctx, {
    x: 0, y: (24 - 2) * HUG.u, u: HUG.u, pose: 'sit', h: 24, clay: parentClay, clayDk: parentDk, ink: G.line,
    eyes: 'open', eyeColor: G.ink, shine: G.white, look: [-0.15, 0.75], arms: null,
  });
  ctx.filter = 'none';
  ctx.restore();
  // ---- the baby, the parent's nub over its shoulder (left) and around its legs (right) ----
  const armY = 6 * br;
  const armC = mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.14);
  // the far nub: out of the parent's side and in behind the baby's back
  hand(ctx, G, { x: 420, y: 1150 + armY + 30 * s.settle, a: Math.PI - 0.12, s: 0.62, color: mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.26) });
  const bx = SELFIE.x - 10 * s.settle, by = SELFIE.y + 62 * s.settle + 5 * br;
  const tilt = -0.05 * s.settle;
  const r = SELFIE.r;
  // the bottle: from below to the mouth, a slow suck, then slipping onto the parent's arm
  const sip = 0.025 * Math.sin((C.t - C.start) * 5.2) * (1 - s.slip);
  const P0 = { x: 520, y: 1000, a: -0.15 }, P1 = { x: 95, y: 268, a: -0.62 + sip }, P2 = { x: 330, y: 560, a: 0.62 };
  const lp = (a, b, k) => ({ x: lerp(a.x, b.x, k), y: lerp(a.y, b.y, k), a: lerp(a.a, b.a, k) });
  const pose = lp(lp(P0, P1, s.bottleIn), P2, s.slip);

"""/** Fan angle with speed ramping v0 -> v1 over [a, b] (analytic, so frames stay independent). */
function fanAngle(t, a, b, v0, v1) {
  if (t <= a) return v0 * t;
  const base = v0 * a;
  if (t <= b) { const d = t - a; return base + v0 * d + ((v1 - v0) * d * d) / (2 * (b - a)); }
  return base + v0 * (b - a) + ((v1 - v0) * (b - a)) / 2 + v1 * (t - b);
}

I've redesigned the bridge long take to stage the seven new lyric beats in order, re-rendered the 540p draft, and committed it in two normal commits (3c523ad, then 5a74afc fixing pops found at 30 fps). The smoke test, --check and the loop all pass.

Draft: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-suno-F2-tender-540p.mp4. Your draft-suno-F2-540p.mp4 was not touched.

Render log:

[render] section plate ok: bridge long take -> nap:long 2:11.89-2:37.45 (25.6s)

How the take plays now (scenes/nap.js, mode long):

  • 10:50, "you're holding me": the adult Clawd parent's body fills the background, darker and slightly blurred, with its left edge and the warm room showing. One parent nub goes behind the baby's back and one crosses its legs; the phone nub stays clear. The light is warm and soft, the breathing is slow, and nothing moves on the beat.
  • 10:52, "drinking": the bottle comes up to the mouth and the milk level drops; the baby blushes on "warm".
  • 10:55, "warm, safe, heavy": the eyes get slowly heavy, the baby settles lower and the phone droops a little.
  • 10:58, "I love you": the baby's eyes leave the phone and look up. The parent leans in until its eyes, with highlights, sit just under the status bar, and the look is held.
  • Parenthetical: the caption shows it as usual; the look holds and the eyes get heavier.
  • 11am, "napping": the eyes close and the bottle slips onto the parent's arm. The phone tips up past the parent's face to the ceiling, the fan slows to nearly still, and the frame fades toward warm light.
  • "(I'm safe... I'm warm... I'm napping...)": breathing only until the grid.

Timing and other takes: stage times come from the bridge clauses (time stamp first, then the words "holding", "drinking", "heavy", "love", "napping"), with fallbacks and minimum gaps so stages can't swap order. On the E2 take and the mock timing, the old bridge words still stage cleanly. On E2 the bottle is at the mouth from the first frame and the look-up lands on "10:58 and I'm heavy".

Other changes: I added opt-in options in props.js and baby.js whose defaults reproduce the current drawing. 18 stills from elsewhere in the video, in both 9:16 and 16:9, are pixel-identical to the previous version. The lidSoft eye shortens from the top instead of drawing a lid line. Without it, heavy lids looked unimpressed, and a sagging lid looked angry. The video README and TREATMENT bridge rows are updated.

What still looks off:

  • Until its eyes arrive, the parent reads as a large brown backdrop. The arms and the visible edge help, but its identity really lands at "I love you".
  • The nub across the legs is a straight block (the existing adult-nub prop, props.hand), so it is a bit plank-like.
  • Clawd's eyes are solid shapes, so the look-up is the eyes shifting upward in the face. It is subtle in stills but visible in motion.
  • The eyes open fully again for the look-up before getting heavier, so the heaviness isn't one steady slide. That was my call; capping the reopen would keep it continuous.
  • On the mock timing, 10:58 and 11am are only 1.9 s apart, so the parent is still leaning in as the eyes close.
  • I did not regenerate sheets/plates.png.
  • The second render took 358 s instead of 42 s because the machine was loaded by other processes (load average 24), not because of this change.

Three follow-up fixes to the tender bridge long take, then re-render the 540p draft to the same path (/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-suno-F2-tender-540p.mp4) and commit as normal commits.

I looked at frames at 133, 137, 142, 147, 151, 155 and 157 s. Staging order is right and the "I love you" reveal of the parent's eyes works. What to change:

  1. The parent should read as Clawd from the first frame, not a brown backdrop. Stage it as a real selfie-from-the-lap: the phone is low, pointing up at the baby, so the parent's face is above the baby and the parent's eyes (with highlights) are partially visible at the top of the frame from 10:50 on, looking down at the baby (pupils/highlights down and toward the baby, not at the camera). On "I love you" the baby looks up and the parent leans in: the eyes come lower and larger into the frame and hold, as you have now. Keep the parent's body darker and slightly blurred as background, but let its silhouette show a rounded body edge or shoulder curve so it is clearly a body, not a wall.
  2. The nub across the baby's legs is a straight plank. Make it a rounded Clawd arm nub (rounded ends, a little shading on the underside) and wrap it under/around the baby so the baby sits in the crook of the arm. The one behind the back can stay. The result should feel like being held, with the baby slightly lower in frame than in the crib shots.
  3. Make the eye heaviness one continuous slide. Cap the reopen at "I love you" so the eyes get no wider than they were at the end of "heavy"; the look-up should be the eyes shifting upward, not reopening. Then they keep closing through the parenthetical to shut at 11am.

Keep everything else as is (bottle, blush, tip-over, fan settle, fade). Keep determinism, both layouts, smoke and --check passing. Check frames at the same seven times after rendering, iterate if the parent still does not read as Clawd at 133 s. Report the draft path and anything still off.

Address this before completing your current task.

// Phone status bar (camera space, top 110 px): clock = the escalating number, a red
// record dot with a running timer, signal and a battery that drains across the day and
// charges (bolt) during naps.

import { clamp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

export const SB = { h: 112, y: 70 };
const SANS = '"Inter", sans-serif';
const MONO = '"IBM Plex Mono", monospace';

/** 12-hour, no am/pm, no leading zero: the way a phone shows it. */
export function phoneClock(min) {
  const m = Math.floor(((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
  const h = Math.floor(m / 60) % 12 || 12;
  return `${h}:${String(m % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
export const recText = (secs) => `${String(Math.floor(secs / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.floor(secs % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export function drawStatusBar(ctx, { min, battery, rec, alpha = 1, dark = false }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  // a soft scrim so white glyphs read on a white noon wall
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 190);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.34)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1080, 190);
  const fg = dark ? '#111111' : '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillStyle = fg;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  // clock
  ctx.font = `700 46px ${SANS}`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(phoneClock(min), 74, SB.y + 6);
  // record pill (centre)
  if (rec && rec.on) {
    ctx.font = `600 36px ${MONO}`;
    const txt = recText(rec.secs);

// the lean (its eyes are in the top of the frame from the first shot, larger and lower on the lean)
const HUG = { u: 100, x: 640, ey0: 70, ey1: 250, grow: 0.18 };

/** An adult Clawd arm nub along a curve (round ends, darker underside): the arm the baby sits in. */
function cradleNub(ctx, G, [x0, y0], [cx, cy], [x1, y1], t, clay) {
  const path = (dy) => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y0 + dy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy + dy, x1, y1 + dy); };
  ctx.save();
  ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  path(0); ctx.strokeStyle = G.line; ctx.lineWidth = t + 18; ctx.stroke();
  path(0); ctx.strokeStyle = mix(clay, '#2e120c', 0.3); ctx.lineWidth = t; ctx.stroke();
  path(-0.12 * t); ctx.strokeStyle = clay; ctx.lineWidth = 0.74 * t; ctx.stroke();
  path(-0.3 * t); ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.4), 0.3); ctx.lineWidth = 0.14 * t; ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}""")
rep("""  const top = lerp(HUG.top0, HUG.top1, s.lean) - 30 * s.settle + 8 * br;
  const ey = top + 2 * HUG.u;
  const m = ctx.getTransform();
  const px = Math.hypot(m.a, m.b);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(HUG.x, ey);
  ctx.rotate(-0.03 * s.lean);
  ctx.scale(1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br, 1 + 0.05 * s.lean + 0.004 * br);
  ctx.filter = `blur(${(2 * px).toFixed(2)}px)`;
  adultClawd(ctx, {
    x: 0, y: (24 - 2) * HUG.u, u: HUG.u, pose: 'sit', h: 24, clay: parentClay, clayDk: parentDk, ink: G.line,
    eyes: 'open', eyeColor: G.ink, shine: G.white, look: [-0.15, 0.75], arms: null,
  });""","""  const ey = lerp(HUG.ey0, HUG.ey1, s.lean) - 30 * s.settle + 8 * br;
  const m = ctx.getTransform();
  const px = Math.hypot(m.a, m.b);
  const sc = 1 + HUG.grow * s.lean + 0.004 * br;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(HUG.x, ey);
  ctx.rotate(-0.04 - 0.03 * s.lean);   // the head tipped toward the baby
  ctx.scale(sc, sc);
  ctx.filter = `blur(${(2 * px).toFixed(2)}px)`;
  // looking down at the baby (its face is below and a little left of the parent's centre)
  adultClawd(ctx, {
    x: 0, y: (24 - 2) * HUG.u, u: HUG.u, pose: 'sit', h: 24, clay: parentClay, clayDk: parentDk, ink: G.line,
    eyes: 'open', eyeColor: G.ink, shine: G.white, look: [-0.35, 1.0], arms: null, shoulder: { k: 1.5, q: 0.9, at: 6 },
  });""")
rep("""  const armY = 6 * br;
  const armC = mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.14);
  // the far nub: out of the parent's side and in behind the baby's back
  hand(ctx, G, { x: 420, y: 1150 + armY + 30 * s.settle, a: Math.PI - 0.12, s: 0.62, color: mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.26) });
  const bx = SELFIE.x - 10 * s.settle, by = SELFIE.y + 62 * s.settle + 5 * br;""","""  const armY = 6 * br + 15 * s.settle;
  const armC = mix(G.parentSkin, '#3a1a14', 0.14);

  // held: lower in the frame than the crib shots, sinking into the arm as it gets heavy
  const bx = SELFIE.x - 10 * s.settle, by = SELFIE.y + 70 + 50 * s.settle + 5 * br;""")
rep("""  hand(ctx, G, { x: 330, y: 1650 + armY, a: 0.5, s: 1.0, color: armC });""",
"""  // the near nub: in from the right, under the baby's bottom and up its far side (the crook it sits in)

def edit(p, pairs):
    s=open(p).read()
    for a,b in pairs:
        assert s.count(a)==1, (p, a)
        s=s.replace(a,b)
    open(p,'w').write(s)

async function encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson) {
  const from = Number(opt('from', 0));
  const to = Math.min(Number(opt('to', info.duration)), info.duration);
  const f0 = Math.round(from * fps), f1 = Math.round(to * fps);
  const N = f1 - f0;
  const audio = pickAudio(audioJson);
  log(`audio: ${path.relative(REPO, audio.file)} (${audio.why}, ${audio.d.toFixed(2)}s)`);
  const out = abs(outMp4);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  const ff = spawn('ffmpeg', [
    '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-stats_period', '5',
    '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgba', '-s', `${w}x${h}`, '-r', String(fps), '-i', 'pipe:0',
    '-ss', String(from), '-t', String(N / fps), '-i', audio.file,
    '-map', '0:v', '-map', '1:a',
    '-vf', 'scale=out_color_matrix=bt709:out_range=tv,format=yuv420p,setparams=color_primaries=bt709:color_trc=bt709:colorspace=bt709:range=tv',
    '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', opt('preset', 'medium'), '-crf', String(opt('crf', 16)),
    '-colorspace', 'bt709', '-color_primaries', 'bt709', '-color_trc', 'bt709', '-color_range', 'tv',
    '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-shortest', '-movflags', '+faststart', out,
  ], { stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
  const ffDone = new Promise((res, rej) => ff.on('close', (c) => (c === 0 ? res() : rej(new Error('ffmpeg exited ' + c)))));

  const buffered = new Map();
  let next = f0, dispatched = f0, written = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  let paintMs = 0;
  const BATCH = 4;
  const maxAhead = workers.length * BATCH * 3;

Redesign tender bridge scene final report6:24 PM

I've made all three of your fixes and re-rendered the 540p draft. Smoke and --check pass for both layouts and both takes. The new draft is at the same path: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-suno-F2-tender-540p.mp4 (208.36 s). Committed as 7a1318d, on top of 3c523ad and 5a74afc.

What changed:

  1. The parent reads as Clawd at 133 s. Its tall eyes, with highlights, sit at the top of the frame from 10:50 on, looking down and toward the baby. The body stays darker and slightly blurred, but its edge is now a rounded shoulder curve with the room showing beside it, so it reads as a body rather than a wall. On "I love you" the parent leans in and its eyes come lower and about 18% larger, then hold. The rounded shape is a new option on adultClawd that only the bridge uses.
  2. The plank is now a rounded arm. It has round ends and a darker underside, comes in from the right, runs under the baby's bottom and up its far side, so the baby sits in the crook of the arm. The baby sits 70 px lower than in the crib shots and sinks a little further as it gets heavy. The nub behind the back is unchanged.
  3. The eyes close in one continuous slide. They get heavy through 10:55, then a little heavier through "I love you" with no reopening. The look-up is the eyes moving up the face; I made that shift larger so it still reads on half-shut eyes. They keep closing through the parenthetical and are shut at 11am. Eye size only decreases from 144.8 s to 153.2 s.

The bottle, blush, tip-over, fan settle and fade are unchanged.

Checks:

  • Frames at 133, 137, 142, 147, 151, 155 and 157 s from the new mp4 look right. 16:9, the E2 take and the mock timing also stage correctly.
  • The 4am, 12pm and 10pm parent scenes are still pixel-identical to the original.

  • Render log:

    [render] section plate ok: bridge long take -> nap:long 2:11.89-2:37.45 (25.6s)
[render] section plate ok: final chorus grid -> grid 2:37.45-3:23.08 (45.6s)
[render] section plate ok: outro loop -> hello 3:23.08-3:28.36 (5.3s)
  • No headless Chrome is left running.

Two failed renders, now resolved: two full renders died with a Chrome timeout. The Mac is a laptop on battery and was going to sleep in the middle of them (the power log shows it waking from deep idle). For a while the draft path held an incomplete 206 s file. Rendering under caffeinate -i then finished in 42 s. The new scene is not slower: bridge frames render as fast as verse frames.

Still off:

  • At 10:50 the parent's eyes sit under the status bar and its overlay, so they read a bit dim until the lean.
  • The new arm reads as a held arm but is a little sausage-like; its far end comes from off-frame right, so you never see it join the body.
  • With half-shut eyes, the look-up is the eyes rising toward the forehead. It reads in motion, but at 151 to 153 s the narrow slits looking up come close to a sleepy side-glance.
  • On the mock timing, 10:58 and 11am are 1.9 s apart, so the lean is still finishing as the eyes close.
  • I did not regenerate sheets/plates.png.
